### [1] [Learning Nonlinear Factor Models with Unknown Monotone Links from Incomplete and Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26271)
*Yutong Chao,Resat Gökhan,Jalal Etesami,Ali Habibnia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非线性因子模型，通过RKHS建模未知单调链接函数，并采用投影块坐标下降算法联合恢复低秩因子、载荷和非线性链接函数，在无噪声和有噪声场景下均建立了收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性因子模型无法处理非线性依赖关系，而现有非线性方法面临严重的非凸性和可识别性问题，该问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将未知单调链接函数假设在RKHS中以保证可识别性，提出带显式正则化的投影块坐标下降（BCD）算法，交替更新低秩因子、载荷和非线性链接函数。

Result: 在温和的因子非相干性和标准采样条件下，建立了无噪声和有噪声场景下的收敛保证，以及链接函数更新的次线性遗憾界；合成实验表明方法具有良好性能。

Conclusion: 将经典线性因子模型推广到广泛的非线性场景，为学习非线性潜在结构提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: We study a nonlinear factor model in which observed responses depend on low-rank latent factors through an unknown monotone link function. This setting is challenging and largely underexplored due to severe nonconvexity and identifiability issues. The link function is assumed to lie in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS), enabling flexible nonparametric modeling while preserving identifiability. We formulate the problem as the joint recovery of the low-rank factors, loadings, and the nonlinear link function from possibly incomplete and noisy observations and propose a projected block coordinate descent (BCD) algorithm with explicit regularization to address scale and rotational ambiguities. Under mild incoherence of factors and standard sampling conditions, we establish convergence guarantees in both noiseless and noisy regimes, along with sublinear regret bounds for the link-function updates. Our results extend classical linear factor models to a broad nonlinear regime and provide a principled framework for learning nonlinear latent structures. We evaluate the proposed approach using controlled synthetic experiments, indicating promising performance.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Differences: Doubly Robust Meta-Learners for Ratio-Based Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26288)
*Michael Fuchs,Dominik Kreiss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Learner方法将比率CATE分解为两个优势比的乘积，将二元结果的比率CATE估计转化为两个倾向性分类任务，并推导了双重稳健增强版本。在七个RCT数据集上，Q-Learner在低转化率场景下表现最稳健；在四个观测数据集上，双重稳健学习器表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当处理效应以比率形式表达时（如医学、定价、营销），比率CATE τ(x)=E[Y|W=1,X=x]/E[Y|W=0,X=x]是合适的估计量，但现有方法要么强加对数线性参数结构，要么使用通用回归且对此泛函缺乏鲁棒性保证。

Method: 将τ(x)分解为两个优势比的乘积，将二元结果的比率CATE估计转化为两个倾向性分类任务。进一步推导了S/T-和Q-风格比率学习器的双重稳健增强版本。

Result: 在七个RCT数据集上，Q-Learner在低转化率场景下表现最稳健；在四个观测数据集上，双重稳健学习器表现最优。

Conclusion: Q-Learner通过倾向性构建避免了结果估计器在不平衡回归上的问题；双重稳健学习器是对抗混杂的观测数据的自然默认选择。

Abstract: When treatment effects are naturally expressed as ratios -- as in medicine, pricing, and marketing -- the ratio-based CATE $τ(x) = E[Y|W=1,X=x] / E[Y|W=0,X=x]$ is the appropriate estimand. Yet existing estimators either impose a log-linear parametric structure or apply generic regression without robustness guarantees for this functional. We introduce the Q-Learner, which decomposes $τ(x)$ into a product of two odds ratios, reducing ratio-CATE estimation for binary outcomes to two propensity classification tasks. We further derive doubly robust augmentations for both S/T- and Q-style ratio learners and characterize their distinct robustness properties. In benchmarks on seven RCT datasets, the Q-Learner is the most consistently competitive method in low-conversion regimes, where its propensity-only construction sidesteps the imbalanced regression that hurts outcome-based estimators. On four observational datasets, where propensity must be estimated and confounding cannot be ruled out, the DR learners introduced here decisively come out on top, making them practitioners' natural default for confounded observational data.

</details>


### [3] [When Does LeJEPA Learn a World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26379)
*David Klindt,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明LeJEPA（对齐加高斯正则化）在隐变量服从平稳加性噪声转移的一类广泛世界中，能够线性可识别地恢复世界的潜在变量，且高斯分布是实现这一保证的唯一潜分布。理论包含正向谱分解证明、逆向唯一性证明、近似可识别性以及线性正交可识别性支持最优潜空间规划，实验从2D到1024维潜变量验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在可靠规划和组合泛化方面存在不足，因为混淆真实自由度的表征无法支持这些能力。本文旨在为经验上成功的LeJEPA方法提供数学保证，证明其能线性恢复潜在变量，从而为构建可证明恢复世界结构的世界模型奠定理论基础。

Method: 1) 正向证明：利用谱分解，使得每个非线性度都被对齐严格惩罚，从而线性映射成为最优解；2) 反向证明：排除所有非高斯潜分布的可能性；3) 近似可识别性：证明在偏离理想条件时保证会优雅退化；4) 线性正交可识别性：证明其支持最优潜空间规划。

Result: 1) LeJEPA在隐变量服从平稳加性噪声转移的世界中实现线性可识别性；2) 高斯分布是实现该保证的唯一潜分布；3) 近似可识别性保证可优雅退化；4) 线性正交可识别性支持最优潜空间规划；5) 从2D到1024维潜变量的实验（含分布消融和基于像素的机器人控制）验证了理论。

Conclusion: 本文将经验成功的LeJEPA方法转化为数学保证，证明其能够可证明地恢复世界的潜在结构，为构建可靠的世界模型提供了理论基石。

Abstract: A representation that scrambles the true degrees of freedom of the world cannot support reliable planning or compositional generalization. We prove that LeJEPA (alignment plus Gaussian regularization) linearly recovers the world's latent variables from nonlinear observations, a property known as linear identifiability, in a broad class of worlds where latents evolve under stationary, additive-noise transitions. Our main result is that among all such worlds, the Gaussian is the unique latent distribution for which this guarantee holds. The forward direction rests on a spectral decomposition in which each degree of nonlinearity is strictly penalized by alignment, making the linear map the optimum; the converse rules out every non-Gaussian alternative. We further prove an approximate identifiability result where the guarantee degrades gracefully, and show that linear, orthogonal identifiability enables optimal latent-space planning. We validate the theory with experiments ranging from 2D examples to 1024-dimensional latents, including distributional ablations and pixel-based robotic control. Our theory turns an empirically successful recipe into a mathematical guarantee, providing the foundation for building World Models that provably recover the structure of the world.

</details>


### [4] [CART Random Forests as Sequential Allocation over Random Opportunity Sets: A Stochastic-Control Theory of Ensemble Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26675)
*Tianxing Mei,Yingying Fan,Mingming Leng,Jinchi Lv*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从随机控制视角重新诠释CART随机森林，将特征子抽样视为随机可行动作集，CART分裂规则视为掩码动作分配策略，建立了分裂计数状态的受控随机过程，从而打开CART森林黑箱，分离出特征子抽样率与掩码内分裂策略两个设计杠杆，并证明了CART策略的局部稳定性与全局次优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CART随机森林虽广泛使用且经验成功，但其机制层面因复杂性常被视为黑箱，缺乏对其内部工作机制的理论理解。

Method: 提出CART-ROSA框架，将特征子抽样解释为随机可行动作集，CART分裂规则解释为掩码动作分配策略，将其建模为分裂计数状态的受控随机过程，并通过线性模型特例推导MSE风险展开。

Result: CART策略具有局部稳定性——能收缩信息性分裂分配的不平衡并集中终端树几何结构，但系统层面对于森林目标可能是全局次优的。在线性模型下显式推导了MSE风险展开。

Conclusion: 运筹学视角使从标准算法描述难以触及的理论缺口变得可处理，为理解CART森林内部机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: CART random forests are among the most widely used modern predictive methods, with well-documented empirical success. Yet, at the mechanistic level, the algorithm is often treated as a black box because of its complexity. In this paper, we develop a stochastic-control perspective on feature-subsampled CART random forests, named CART random opportunity-set allocation (CART-ROSA). At each node, the random subset of features is interpreted as a random feasible action set, and the CART split rule as a masked-action allocation policy. This policy induces a controlled stochastic process over informative split-count states, whose terminal law determines both single-tree error and cross-tree interaction terms in the forest mean squared error (MSE). Such representation opens the black box of CART-forests by separating two design levers: the informative-opportunity rate induced by feature subsampling, and the contraction strength from the within-mask split policy. We establish that the CART policy is locally stabilizing: it contracts imbalances in informative split allocations and concentrates terminal tree geometry. At the system level, however, it can be globally suboptimal for the forest objective. Specializing to the linear model, we derive the MSE risk expansion explicitly. Our results show how an operations-research perspective makes tractable a theoretical gap difficult to access from the standard algorithmic description of CART forests.

</details>


### [5] [Transformers Can Learn Posterior Predictive Distributions In-Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26713)
*Gyeonghun Kang,Changwoo J. Lee,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度证明Transformer可以通过注意力机制实现梯度下降算法来近似高斯过程回归的后验预测分布（PPD），并分析了归一化与注意力深度在泛化能力中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先验数据拟合网络（PFNs）在贝叶斯预测任务中表现出强大的经验性能，但关于Transformer在上下文中学习分布的理论理解仍然缺乏。

Method: 通过构造方法证明Transformer能实现逼近后验预测均值和方差的梯度下降算法，随后通过非线性映射得到PPD的分箱概率；分析了近似PPD的误差界与注意力深度和分箱分辨率的关系。

Result: 给出了Transformer近似PPD的误差界；证明了归一化和注意力深度选择对于模型在预训练样本量范围外实现外推能力的关键作用。

Conclusion: 本研究为PFNs在表达后验预测分布方面的能力提供了理论洞见，并揭示了架构选择如何影响泛化性能。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) have recently emerged as a powerful approach for Bayesian prediction tasks, approximating the posterior predictive distribution (PPD) through in-context learning. Despite their strong empirical performance and ability to go beyond point predictions, theoretical understandings of the algorithmic capability of transformers to learn distributions in context are still lacking. Focusing on Gaussian process regression problems, we show by construction that transformers can implement a gradient descent algorithm targeting the posterior predictive mean and variance, followed by nonlinear mappings that yield binned probabilities of PPD. We study the error bounds of the approximated PPD in terms of attention depth and bin resolution. Based on these results, we further demonstrate the key role of normalization and the choice of attention depth in enabling the extrapolation abilities of transformers beyond the pretraining sample size range. We conduct simulations that corroborate our findings, providing insight into the expressivity of PFNs targeting PPDs and how architectural choices may influence generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [6] [Signal-to-Noise Ratio and Sample Size Govern Representational Alignment in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26973)
*Ali Hussaini Umar,Alessandro Laio*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络中潜在表征的"对齐"现象，发现信噪比和训练样本量分别以单调和非单调方式影响对齐程度，且对齐在插值阈值附近最小化，与泛化性能无直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络在不同架构、训练协议或数据集下形成的潜在表征对齐现象，以及其与数据质量、数量和泛化性能之间的关系。

Method: 在受控条件下训练集成网络，对回归和分类任务使用独立噪声扰动的训练集；引入单隐层线性网络进行解析分析，并与真实数据集上的非线性网络进行对比。

Result: 对齐程度随信噪比单调变化，但随训练样本量非单调变化；对齐在插值阈值附近最小化；更强的对齐并不对应更好的泛化误差。

Conclusion: 对齐现象与泛化性能解耦，揭示了数据质量和数量对表征结构影响的非平凡依赖关系。

Abstract: Neural networks are known to develop latent representations that are $aligned$, namely structurally similar across networks trained with different architectures, training protocols, or training datasets. We study this phenomenon in a controlled setting, where we train an ensemble of networks on regression and classification tasks using training sets perturbed by independent realizations of a noise process. We show that the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and the training sample size influence the alignment in qualitatively similar ways in networks trained on real-world datasets and in an extremely simple $linear$ network with a single hidden layer, for which the alignment can be estimated analytically. Across linear and nonlinear networks, regression and classification tasks, and both synthetic and real-world data, we consistently observe that alignment varies monotonically with SNR but non-monotonically with training sample size. In particular, the alignment is minimized near the interpolation threshold, and a stronger alignment does not necessarily correspond to better generalization error. These findings reveal a non-trivial dependence of alignment on data quality and quantity, decoupled from generalization performance.

</details>


### [7] [Constrained Bayesian Experimental Design via Online Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26990)
*Yujia Guo,Daolang Huang,Xinyu Zhang,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski,Ayush Bharti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种结合离线预训练摊销策略和后验网络与在线多步前瞻情景树规划的方法，实现对动态约束下的贝叶斯实验设计（BED）的约束优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BED方法无法适应真实任务中因预算限制、成本变化或物理约束导致的动态约束，无法有效约束实验设计随时间的演化。

Method: 离线预训练摊销策略和后验网络，结合在线多步前瞻规划（使用情景树）进行约束优化。

Result: 在多个约束BED任务中，该方法生成的信息量显著优于现有方法，且仅带来适度的额外计算开销。

Conclusion: 所提方法能高效处理带约束的序列实验设计问题，兼顾信息量和计算效率。

Abstract: Bayesian experimental design (BED) is a principled framework for data-efficient design of sequential experiments. However, existing BED methods are unable to adapt to dynamic constraints inherent in real-world tasks due to budget limitations, varying costs, or physical constraints that restrict how designs evolve over time. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach to BED that enables constrained optimization of experimental designs by combining offline pre-training of an amortized policy and a posterior network with online multi-step lookahead planning using scenario trees. We empirically demonstrate that our method yields substantially more informative design sequences than existing methods across a range of constrained BED tasks, while incurring only a modest additional computational overhead.

</details>


### [8] [Causal Representation Learning for Generalisable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27043)
*Yorgos Felekis,Michael O'Riordan,Oriol Corcoll,Ciarán M. Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因果表征学习的信息论解缠标准，通过可计算变分下界优化，仅需观测数据即可提升模型在分布偏移下的泛化能力。在Spotify百万用户A/B测试、KuaiRand数据集及合成基准上，该方法与基线模型离线性能相当，但在线上部署及分布偏移场景下显著提升用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于观测数据训练的预测模型常因训练分布与部署分布不一致而泛化失败，推荐系统尤为典型。训练数据受部署策略、历史用户行为及平台过滤共同混淆，导致离线评估指标无法可靠预测在线性能。现有因果表征学习目标过于宏大（完全识别所有潜在因果因子），缺乏可直接从有限混杂观测数据中优化的实用方法。

Method: 提出信息论解缠标准，理论上证明其最优解仅依赖输入的因果成分；推导出可处理的变分下界，使其能从有限观测数据中优化。该目标比完整因果识别更窄、更实用：仅需现有混杂日志、适用于任意标准监督模型、不增加推理成本。

Result: Spotify个性化歌单排序器百万用户A/B测试：容量匹配的CRL变体离线性能相当，但在线用户参与度显著提升；KuaiRand公开数据集与合成基准实验：离线与基线持平，分布偏移下表现更好。

Conclusion: 添加因果解缠目标能在分布偏移下实现更优的泛化性能，且不牺牲离线表现，是一种实用有效的分布偏移解决方案。

Abstract: Predictive models trained on observational data often fail to generalise to the distributions they encounter when deployed, especially when the training data is a product of the system being optimised. Recommender systems are a canonical example: they are trained on interaction logs confounded by the deployed policy, past user behaviour, and platform filtering. As a result, the training distribution differs substantially from the candidate distribution scored at serving time, a gap that makes offline metrics unreliable predictors of online performance. We address the distribution shift problem with a method motivated by causal representation learning (CRL). We propose an information-theoretic disentanglement criterion and prove that its optimum depends only on the causal components of the input. We then derive a tractable variational lower bound that makes the criterion optimisable from finite observational data alone. The scope of our method is narrower than that of much of the CRL literature, in that we target better generalisation under distribution shift, not full identification of all latent causal factors. This narrower target is what makes the method practical, requiring only the existing confounded logs, applying to any standard supervised model, and adding no inference-time cost. Our headline evaluation is an A/B test with millions of users on Spotify, applied to a production ranker for personalised playlist generation. A capacity-matched CRL variant performed on par offline but delivered substantial online gains in listener engagement. Complementary evidence on the public KuaiRand recommendation dataset and a synthetic benchmark with known causal structure shows the same pattern: offline parity with baseline, gains under distribution shift. Across all three settings, adding our causal disentanglement objective yields meaningfully better out-of-distribution generalisation.

</details>


### [9] [Gaussian Process-based learning with new MCMC-based implementation of Wishart prior on correlation matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27093)
*Kane Warrior,Dalia Chakrabarty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自组装Wishart先验，用于高斯过程学习中协方差矩阵的直接先验指定，通过MCMC的滑动窗口自适应调整尺度矩阵，以解决多长度尺度参数推断困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高斯过程的概率监督学习中，当目标函数高度多元时，需同时学习多个长度尺度参数，导致推断困难。标准做法是为核超参数指定先验，但协方差矩阵受核参数间接控制，缺乏直接先验约束。

Method: 开发了一种"自组装"Wishart先验用于协方差矩阵，在MCMC贝叶斯推断核超参数的同时，利用最近MCMC迭代的滑动窗口定义依赖于时间步的尺度矩阵，引入链的自适应性。

Result: 实验表明，对协方差矩阵的直接先验指定有助于诊断GP学习中的弱信息输入。在合成数据和真实数据集上均得到实证支持。

Conclusion: 直接对协方差矩阵指定先验（而非间接通过核超参数）在高斯过程学习中是有用的，尤其适用于识别弱信息输入维度，且所提的自适应Wishart先验在多元场景下有效。

Abstract: In probabilstic supervised learning of an input-output relationship - as a sample function of a Gaussian Process (GP) - priors are typically specified for the hyperparameters of the kernel that parametrises the covariance function of the GP, where the induced covariance matrix of the (resulting multivariate Normal) likelihood, governs the learning and prediction. When the sought function is highly multivariate, multiple lengthscale parameters must be learnt simultaneously, making inference difficult. We develop a ``self-assembled'' Wishart prior for the covariance matrix, while undertaking Bayesian inference on the kernel hyperparameters using MCMC. The construction uses a look-back window over recent MCMC iterations to define a time-step dependent scale matrix, thereby introducing adaptiveness to the chain. Results suggest that direct prior specification on the covariance matrix can be useful for diagnosing weakly informative inputs within the GP-based learning paradigm. We support our prior development with two distinct empirical illustrations - one on synthetic data, and another on a real-world dataset.

</details>

### [10] [Prox-NAG-GS: A Semi-Implicit Proximal Method for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26260)
*Sikeh Gisele Wiykiynyuy,Kelvin Asu Ekuri,Valentin Leplat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Prox-NAG-GS方法，将NAG-GS的半隐式加速结构扩展到复合优化问题，通过近端步替代原始的第二更新，处理光滑损失与非光滑正则化器的组合目标函数。在强凸复合情形下证明线性收敛，在凸情形下证明O(1/k)速率，并在弹性网、Group Lasso和随机稀疏softmax回归任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

The results demonstrate that the proposed formulation and algorithm produce generalized inverses that are substantially sparser than the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse while maintaining significantly smaller 1-norms than competing approaches. Moreover, the DRS algorithm exhibits superior scalability relative to exact linear-optimization formulations, successfully solving instances far beyond the reach of commercial solvers. Abstract: Policy learning in modern operations environments faces a fundamental tension between limited operational data and the large, often continuous, state and action spaces over which good decisions must be identified and deployed. We study value-based policy learning in stochastic optimal control: a greedy policy induced by an estimate of the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ is deployed, and its performance is measured by regret. The empirical success of this approach calls for statistical insight into the structures that enable fast regret convergence. We show that, in continuous action spaces, fast policy learning is induced by three geometric structures: a growth exponent $p$, which quantifies how quickly $Q^*$ separates suboptimal actions from its maximizers; a margin-mass exponent $m$, which controls how much deployment mass lies on states with weak growth; and an action-wise regularity exponent $q$, which measures the smoothness of the $Q^*$-estimation error across actions. Given a $n^{-1/2}$-accurate estimator of $Q^*$, we show that the minimax-optimal policy regret convergence rate is \[
  \widetildeΘ\left(
  n^{-\min\left\{\frac{p}{2(p-q)},\frac{m+1}{2m}\right\}}

This regime is natural in operations applications. In particular, we verify $q>0$ under mild regularity conditions in dynamic inventory control and service allocation examples, while the mechanism underlying this fast rate regime extends beyond these settings.

After first-stage outcomes are observed, the second stage determines scenario-dependent recourse projects, including follow-up appraisal, reserve upgrading, conversion-to-proved reserves, rolling extension, and data re-evaluation projects. Abstract: This paper investigates the privacy-preserving distributed Nash equilibrium seeking problem for aggregative games. Abstract: Quadratically regularized optimal transport (QOT) is an alternative to entropic regularization that yields sparse couplings and avoids numerical instabilities due to exponential scaling. Abstract: We consider the problem of determining feasible systems from a finite set of simulated alternatives with respect to probability constraints, where the observations from stochastic simulations are Bernoulli distributed. Although these procedures can be adapted to Bernoulli-distributed data by treating batch means as basic observations, achieving approximate normality often requires a large batch size, potentially leading to the unnecessary waste of observations in reaching a decision. This paper proposes a procedure that utilizes the Bernoulli-distributed observations directly to determine feasibility. In addition, we incorporate subjective constraints, allowing for multiple thresholds for each constraint. We demonstrate that our proposed procedure is statistically valid and that it outperforms an existing feasibility determination procedure for subjective constraints originally developed for normally distributed observations. Furthermore, we propose two heuristic feasibility check approaches for thresholds that are sequentially added by decision makers, allowing thresholds to be tightened when many systems are feasible or relaxed when no feasible system exists. Abstract: In many operational contexts, solutions to NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems, modeled by means of Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), may become infeasible due to unpredictable disruptions. Typically, reoptimizing by solving the MILP formulation on the perturbed instance is not possible as new solutions must be obtained in a very short computing time, while simple repairing heuristics may result in low-quality solutions. To bridge this gap, we propose a learning-to-reoptimize framework, and apply it to the Lot Sizing Problem (LSP) under machine breakdown disruptions. We design a fix-and-optimize strategy aided by a Graph Neural Network (GNN) that efficiently computes a new solution within the neighborhood of a repaired solution. By representing the instance, the original solution and the disruption as a feature graph, we train a GNN to predict the likelihood that specific binary variables require to be modified. These predictions guide the selection of a small subset of variables to be reoptimized by an MILP solver, while the other variables are hard-fixed. Numerical experiments on a large dataset demonstrate that our approach handles effectively different problem sizes, and that it significantly outperforms a baseline alternative approach, yielding larger cost reductions within the same limited time budget.

### [25] [Stochastic global optimization of continuous functions via random walks on Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14151)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于格拉斯曼流形随机游走的随机全局优化方法，通过反复采样低维子空间并求解其上的受限优化问题，实现对高维目标函数的全局最小化。该方法不依赖凸性、光滑性等传统假设，其收敛性仅依赖于通过当前点的k维子空间上受限极小值的几何分布，并引入类似谱间隙的"间隙参数"控制收敛速率。此外，该方法具有盲点鲁棒性：对窄而深的极小值区域（测度小的"向下尖峰"）不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局优化方法通常依赖凸性、光滑性、Lipschitz界或Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件等强假设，这些假设在许多实际高维问题中难以满足。作者希望提出一种不依赖这些经典条件的全局优化方法，仅基于子空间上受限极小值的几何分布给出收敛保证，并同时具备对罕见但深陷的局部极小值的鲁棒性。

Method: 方法的核心思想是在格拉斯曼流形上进行随机游走：在每次迭代中，从当前点出发随机采样一个k维线性子空间（k远小于d），将原始优化问题限制在该子空间上得到低维问题，使用任意黑箱优化器求解该低维问题，然后更新当前迭代点。通过单调改进性质保证每次迭代不劣于前一次。收敛性分析基于通过当前点的所有k维子空间上受限极小值的几何分布，并定义了一个"间隙参数"（类比随机游走的谱间隙）来控制收敛速率。

Result: 1. 给出了不依赖凸性、光滑性等传统条件的收敛性保证，仅依赖于受限极小值的几何分布。2. 识别出控制收敛速率的"间隙参数"。3. 证明了盲点鲁棒性：测度较小但深度较大的极小值区域对算法轨迹影响有限，因为随机子空间采样不太可能遇上这些区域。

Conclusion: 该工作为全局优化提供了一种新的理论分析范式，摆脱了对经典光滑/凸性假设的依赖，转而利用子空间采样策略的几何特性。盲点鲁棒性表明该方法适用于存在稀疏但深度极小值的复杂损失景观。未来可探讨间隙参数与问题结构的具体关系及子空间维数k的自适应选择。

Abstract: We introduce a stochastic global optimization method based on random walks on Grassmannian manifolds. To minimize a continuous objective $\ell:\mathbb{R}^d\rightarrow\mathbb{R}$, the method repeatedly samples random $k$-dimensional linear subspaces (with $k\ll d$), solves the resulting low-dimensional restrictions of these problems to these subspaces using an arbitrary black-box optimizer, and updates the iterate (which monotonically improves upon the previous iterate). Unlike classical optimization analyses that rely on convexity, smoothness, Lipschitz bounds, or Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type conditions, our convergence guarantees depend only on the geometric distribution of restricted minima across the $k$-dimensional subspaces passing through a given point in $\mathbb{R}^d$. We identify a gap parameter -- an analogue of a spectral gap for random walks -- that controls the rate at which the iterates approach the global minimum value. Finally, we argue that the same analysis yields a blind-spot robustness property: sufficiently narrow, deep dips of the loss function (small-measure regions where $\ell$ spikes downward) have limited influence on the algorithm's trajectory, since they are unlikely to be encountered by random subspace sampling.

</details>

### [26] [GEM: Geometric Entropy Mixing for Optimal LLM Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26121)
*Yue Min,Ziyun Qiao,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM（几何熵混合）通过将数据配比重新表述为超球面上的变分问题，并利用MM算法优化混合平衡正则化器，有效克服了嵌入各向异性和聚类坍缩问题，在1.1B参数模型上实现了最高1.2%的下游精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合方法受限于分类缺陷：人工分类存在本体论错位，欧氏聚类无法处理嵌入各向异性，导致难以发现最优数据配比。

Method: 将数据配比构建为超球面上的变分问题，引入混合平衡正则化器；通过解耦生成先验并利用可证明的MM（Minorize-Maximize）算法优化目标；采用师生蒸馏实现网络规模扩展，并提出几何影响分数（GIS）用于可解释的类别生成。

Result: 在1.1B参数模型上，GEM集成到DoReMi和RegMix等混合策略中，平均下游准确率提升最高达1.2%，建立了新的最优性能。

Conclusion: GEM提供了一个鲁棒的坐标系用于可预测的数据混合，证明了几何保真度在大规模数据配比优化中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM pre-training efficacy increasingly depends on data composition rather than sheer volume. Yet, optimal mixing is hindered by categorization flaws: human taxonomies suffer from ontological misalignment, and Euclidean clustering fails to address embedding anisotropy. We introduce GEM (Geometric Entropy Mixing), a framework reformulating data curation as a variational problem on the hypersphere augmented with a mixing-balance regularizer. By decoupling the generative prior and optimizing the objective via a provable MM (Minorize-Maximize) algorithm, GEM effectively counteracts the cluster collapse to discover balanced semantic structures invisible to Euclidean heuristics. We employ teacher-student distillation to scale this geometric fidelity to web-scale corpora and introduce the Geometric Influence Score (GIS) for interpretable taxonomy generation. Experiments with 1.1B-parameter models demonstrate that GEM establishes a new state-of-the-art when integrated into mixing strategies like DoReMi and RegMix, improving average downstream accuracy by up to 1.2% and offering a robust coordinate system for predictable data mixing.

</details>


### [27] [The Constraint Tax: Measuring Validity-Correctness Tradeoffs in Structured Outputs for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26128)
*Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了在小型语言模型（<3B参数）部署中，结构化输出约束会显著降低答案准确性和可执行准确性，提出了"约束税"测量协议，并建议采用"先自由推理，后约束封装"的设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的工程假设认为硬输出约束在不改变答案质量的前提下提升了可靠性，但这一假设对小型模型是不安全的。论文旨在量化和分析结构化约束对SLM答案准确性的负面影响。

Method: 提出"约束税"（constraint tax）测量协议，隔离由结构化输出约束导致的答案准确性和可执行准确性损失。在三个子3B参数模型（Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, SmolLM2-1.7B）上进行15,000次商品GPU生成实验，对比无约束自由生成与硬性schema约束解码的性能差异。

Result: 硬性schema解码将schema有效性从61.5%提升至100.0%，但答案准确性从19.7%降至11.0%，错误有效schema输出从49.5%升至88.9%。在日历工具调用任务中，Qwen2.5-1.5B在prompt-only JSON下可执行准确率为91.5%，但在硬工具调用schema下仅为48.0%。3B边界模型仍存在直接schema税。

Conclusion: 系统应分开报告schema有效性、答案准确性、可执行准确性和错误有效schema率。推荐"先自由推理，后约束封装"（reason free, constrain late）的设计模式以缓解约束税。

Abstract: Production LLM systems increasingly require machine-readable outputs: JSON objects, typed traces, regex-constrained fields, and tool-call schemas. This paper targets on-device and low-cost small language model (SLM) deployments, where sub-3B models are attractive for privacy, latency, and commodity hardware but have limited capacity to satisfy schemas while solving tasks. The usual engineering assumption is that hard output constraints improve reliability without changing the underlying answer. We show that this assumption is unsafe for small models. We introduce \emph{constraint tax}, a measurement protocol for isolating the answer and executable-accuracy loss caused by structured-output constraints at fixed model, fixed task distribution, and fixed problem instances. Across 15,000 commodity-GPU generations with Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and SmolLM2-1.7B, hard answer-only schema decoding raises schema validity from 61.5\% to 100.0\%, but lowers answer accuracy from 19.7\% to 11.0\% and increases wrong-valid-schema outputs from 49.5\% to 88.9\%. The strongest industry analogue is a deterministic calendar tool-call task: Qwen2.5-1.5B achieves 91.5\% executable accuracy with prompt-only JSON but only 48.0\% under the same hard tool-call schema, while both modes are 100.0\% schema-valid. The error is semantic, not structural. We also show that the 3B boundary still pays a direct-schema tax and that delayed packaging supports a constructive design pattern: reason free, constrain late. The practical conclusion is direct: production systems should report schema validity, answer accuracy, executable accuracy, and wrong-valid-schema rate separately.

</details>


### [28] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EarthMind-SR（文中实际名称为AirCast-SR，存在笔误），一种基于潜在一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架的大气超分基础模型，将全球AI天气预报从0.25度（~28 km）降尺度至1 km/1小时分辨率，在美国CONUS区域训练并在印度和德国实现零样本全球迁移，验证了公里级AI气象预报的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报（NWP）在公里尺度上计算成本过高，限制了能源、农业和灾害管理等应用对高时空分辨率预报的获取。

Method: 采用三维U-Net架构，嵌入潜在一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架；以GraphCast预报为输入，NOAA的AORC再分析数据为目标，在CONUS区域进行基于补丁的样本训练，实现8个耦合地表变量同时降尺度。

Result: 所有变量和预报时效上实现近零偏差；径向功率谱密度分析证明在10-100 km波长范围内保留了精细大气结构；在美国冬、夏、春三个案例中验证性能，并在印度和德国实现零样本全球迁移。

Conclusion: 作为开源权重的基础模型，EarthMind-SR建立了公里级AI天气预报的新范式，为区域微调、蒸馏及气候服务和灾害预报等下游应用提供了平台。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [29] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SilIF 通过在 Isolation Forest 的基础上增加基于轮廓系数的评分层来提升无监督异常检测性能，在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测基准上 AUC-PR 提升 0.0080，但在合成数据集上无改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交易欺诈检测等无监督异常检测场景中，标签稀缺且 Isolation Forest 虽广泛使用但性能有限，作者希望在不破坏 IF 易部署性和可扩展性的前提下，通过引入结构信息来增强其检测能力。

Method: SilIF 在 Isolation Forest 的每棵树中提取每个点的路径长度向量作为"指纹"，对这些指纹进行聚类得到结构分组，然后计算轮廓系数来衡量点与其所属组及最近邻组的匹配程度。最终通过超参数 α 将轮廓信号与基础 IF 分数加权融合。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS 基准（约59万笔交易，3.5%欺诈率）上，α=1.0 时 SilIF 比原始 IF 平均 AUC-PR 提升 0.0080，所有五个种子均获胜（配对 t 检验 p=0.046）；在 Sparkov 合成信用卡数据集上未观察到改进。

Conclusion: SilIF 是一种可调参、易部署的 Isolation Forest 增强方案，论文诚实报告了其在何种条件下有效或无效，并开源了代码。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [30] [Neural Bayesian Sequential Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26147)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经贝叶斯顺序路由（NBSR）框架，将神经推理建模为层级有向无环图上的主动证据累积过程，利用狄利克雷- categorical共轭框架实现不确定性感知的路径路由与早期退出，兼具可解释性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络依赖静态密集前向计算，缺乏对证据获取过程、不确定性演化以及计算何时停止的显式建模，与人类顺序且不确定性感知的决策方式不符。

Method: 在层级DAG上，神经专家通过查询全局知识语料库提取正证据向量作为伪计数，通过共轭加法更新狄利克雷信念状态；结合Gumbel-Softmax直通估计器实现硬路由选择与端到端训练；利用狄利克雷精度和熵实现不确定性量化、早期退出、OOD拒绝和成本感知的证据获取。

Result: 在视觉分类、结构化医学诊断、语言建模、部分可观测控制及贝叶斯实验设计等多个任务上，NBSR达到竞争性预测性能，同时提供透明的路由轨迹、路径相关证据归因、不确定性感知决策控制及资源理性推理。

Conclusion: NBSR为可解释、模块化且资源理性的智能体AI提供了一个数学基础扎实的框架。

Abstract: Human decision-making is sequential and uncertainty-aware, yet standard neural networks often rely on static, dense forward computation with limited visibility into evidence acquisition, uncertainty evolution, or when computation should stop. We introduce \textbf{Neural Bayesian Sequential Routing (NBSR)}, a framework that models neural inference as active evidence accumulation over a hierarchical Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). Within a Dirichlet--Categorical conjugate framework, neural experts query a persistent global knowledge oracle to extract positive evidence vectors, which act as pseudo-counts and update a Dirichlet belief state by exact conjugate addition. Coupled with a Gumbel-Softmax Straight-Through estimator, this update enables hard, path-dependent routing while preserving surrogate gradients for end-to-end training. The resulting Dirichlet precision and entropy provide mechanisms for uncertainty quantification, entropy-based early exiting, OOD abstention, and cost-aware evidence acquisition. We prove that, under strictly positive evidence extraction, total Dirichlet precision increases monotonically along any valid trajectory and marginal predictive variance is bounded, formalizing sequential ``hypothesis sharpening''; under idealized capacity and optimization assumptions, the terminal Dirichlet expectation recovers the Bayes-optimal conditional distribution. Empirical evaluations across visual categorization, structured medical diagnosis, language modeling, partially observable control, and cost-aware Bayesian experimental design show that NBSR achieves competitive predictive performance while providing transparent routing traces, path-dependent evidence attribution, uncertainty-aware decision control, and resource-rational inference. Overall, NBSR offers a mathematically grounded framework for interpretable, modular, and resource-rational agentic AI.

</details>


### [31] [TSFMAudit: Data Contamination Auditing in Forecasting Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26161)
*Hongkai Li,Shifeng Xie,Lefei Shen,Zhuo Li,Mouxiang Chen,Xiaobin Zhang,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的预训练污染审计问题，提出基于探针适应动态的TSFMAudit方法，通过检测数据集的异常高效适应（更快的损失下降和更小的骨干网络变动）来判断是否被预训练数据污染。在6个TSFMs和187个数据集上评估，优于10个从LLM领域移植的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在大规模语料上预训练，评估数据集可能已在预训练中暴露，导致性能估计过于乐观。而时间序列信号的连续性和异质性使得污染审计极具挑战，且缺乏语料文档记录，现有研究尚属空白。

Method: 提出TSFMAudit方法，基于探针适应动态（probe adaptation dynamics）进行污染检测。核心直觉：被污染的数据集在微调探针时表现出异常高效适应——更快的损失下降和更小的骨干网络（backbone）参数变动。利用文档化的训练来源证据作为监督信号进行评估。

Result: 在6个时间序列基础模型和187个数据集上评估TSFMAudit，并与10个从大语言模型（LLM）领域移植的竞争性基线方法进行比较，TSFMAudit表现更优。

Conclusion: TSFMAudit能有效审计TSFMs的预训练数据污染问题，填补了该领域的研究空白，为时间序列模型的可信评估提供了新方法。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly pretrained on large corpora, raising concerns that evaluation datasets may have been exposed during pretraining and thus yield overly optimistic performance estimates. Auditing such contamination is challenging in time series because signals are continuous and heterogeneous, and often lack corpus documentation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study pretraining contamination auditing for TSFMs. We formalize the problem of pretraining contamination auditing for TSFMs and propose TSFMAudit, a method based on probe adaptation dynamics. Our key intuition is that contamination manifests as unusually efficient adaptation: after a fine tuning probe, contaminated datasets tend to exhibit faster loss reduction with smaller backbone movement. We evaluate TSFMAudit on 6 TSFMs and 187 datasets using documented training source evidence as supervision, and compare against 10 competitive baselines adapted from the LLM literature.

</details>


### [32] [Beyond Coefficients: Forecast-Necessity Testing for Interpretable Causal Discovery in Nonlinear Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18751)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过预测必要性测试（而非系数大小）来评估非线性时间序列模型中的因果关系，并基于边消融和预测比较的框架进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性机器学习模型越来越多地被用于发现时间序列中的因果关系，但其输出的因果分数常被错误解释为回归系数，导致误导性的统计显著性声明。

Method: 提出基于系统性边消融（edge ablation）和预测比较的评估框架，通过测试某个候选因果关系是否对准确预测是必需的来判断其因果相关性，并以神经加性向量自回归（NAVAR）为案例模型进行验证。

Result: 发现具有相似因果分数的关系在预测必要性上可能存在巨大差异，原因包括冗余性、时间持久性和体制特异性效应。

Conclusion: 预测必要性测试能够支持更可靠的因果推理，为高风险领域中非线性时间序列模型的解释提供实用指导。

Abstract: Nonlinear machine-learning models are increasingly used to discover causal relationships in time-series data, yet the interpretation of their outputs remains poorly understood. In particular, causal scores produced by regularized neural autoregressive models are often treated as analogues of regression coefficients, leading to misleading claims of statistical significance.
  In this paper, we argue that causal relevance in nonlinear time-series models should be evaluated through forecast necessity rather than coefficient magnitude, and we present a practical evaluation procedure for doing so. We present an interpretable evaluation framework based on systematic edge ablation and forecast comparison, which tests whether a candidate causal relationship is required for accurate prediction. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a case study model, we apply this framework to a real-world case study of democratic development, modeled as a multivariate time series of panel data - democracy indicators across 139 countries. We show that relationships with similar causal scores can differ dramatically in their predictive necessity due to redundancy, temporal persistence, and regime-specific effects.
  Our results demonstrate how forecast-necessity testing supports more reliable causal reasoning in applied AI systems and provides practical guidance for interpreting nonlinear time-series models in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [33] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PushCen-ADFL 是一个通信高效的异步去中心化联邦学习框架，通过质心空间中的通信、聚合与局部稳定耦合设计，在非对称通信和延迟客户端参与下实现稳定训练，相比基线方法在异构数据下准确率提升最多6%，通信成本降低80%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步去中心化联邦学习面临三个核心挑战：频繁的点对点通信开销大、有向拓扑上的异步更新导致聚合偏差、以及非独立同分布数据引发严重的模型漂移。现有方法未能同时有效解决这些问题。

Method: PushCen-ADFL 在共享质心表示空间中耦合通信、聚合与局部稳定三模块：1) 客户端交换质心形式消息并应用保均的 push-sum 混合纠正聚合偏差；2) 轻量级质心正则化锚定在相同质心空间以缓解异构性和陈旧性带来的漂移；3) 有限尺寸的发送者去重缓冲区提升异步不规则到达下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉数据集上的实验表明，PushCen-ADFL 在数据异构下准确率提升最高6%，同时每次推送的通信成本降低超过80%，实现了良好的准确率-通信权衡。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL 证明了通过将通信压缩、保均聚合和局部稳定正则化统一在同一质心空间中，可以有效解决 ADFL 中的通信效率、聚合偏差和模型漂移问题，为大规模异构系统提供了一种实用的去中心化训练方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [34] [From Privacy to Generalization: Linear Max-Information Bounds for DP-SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26222)
*Christoph H. Lampert,Hossein Zakerinia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）的近似最大信息量具有与数据集大小线性相关的有限样本界，并由此推导出基于PAC-Bayes的泛化界限，其中先验分布可由DP-SGD学习，同时得到了DP-SGD训练模型的显式泛化界，其复杂度项由优化超参数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解泛化与隐私之间的关系是现代机器学习理论的核心挑战，特别是对于由差分隐私随机梯度下降变体训练的深度网络而言，这一问题尚未解决。

Method: 通过证明DP-SGD的近似最大信息量具有与数据集大小线性相关的有限样本界，建立与经典差分隐私算法相当的性质，并利用该结果推导PAC-Bayes泛化界限。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了隐私与泛化理论之间的鸿沟，为差分隐私深度学习提供了理论保证，且泛化复杂度项完全由优化超参数显式控制。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We propose Lie group embedded dynamical neural networks (LieEDNN) and the corresponding learning algorithms based on gradient descent and metric projection on smooth manifold, where we treat Lie group as an intrinsic representation for continuous symmetry of manifold geometry. Thereby we achieve learnable and stable dynamics on the underlying manifold for general Lie group, and we are able to utilize the powerful representation capability of Lie group such as SO(3) and SE(3) to solve real world engineering problems in areas such as robotics, graphics, and control. Two core challenges are: (i) General Lie groups are incompatible with addition arithmetic, which is necessary for neural network interactions. (ii) The dynamics evolve in the nonlinear representation space of special algebra rather than the normal Euclidean space, which violates the paradigm of common neural ODEs. To address these two challenges, we firstly introduce adjoint Lie group action on the Lie algebra, which induces a linear mapping and transfer to the block-wise structure of weight matrices, such that addition could operate on the Lie algebra as a vector space. Then we parameterize the Lie algebra and the adjoint action as linear transformation so that the architecture is aligned with neural network perceptrons. Explicitly, this embedding appears as block-wise manifold constraints on weights, and we develop algorithms to learn the equilibrium with stability guarantees of the temporal neural network dynamics. Experiments are implemented on a specific Lie group SE(3), with the application scenario of telescopic manipulators.

</details>

*Thien V. Nguyen,Amaury Habrard,Benjamin Guedj*

Motivation: 现有PIML方法在经验上表现优异，但其统计泛化性质尚未被充分理解，尤其是在无界损失的回归设定下。已有的分析依赖近似或稳定性论证，未能充分揭示物理结构如何从有限数据中影响泛化性能。

Result: 推导出两类权衡统计复杂度和光滑性的泛化界；提出的自界感知学习算法在训练过程中可有效最小化所提单界；在标准PDE基准上的实证评估表明所提单界非空泛且显著优于联合界基线。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) integrates mechanistic knowledge, typically in the form of partial differential equations (PDE), into data-driven models. Despite strong empirical performance, its statistical generalisation properties remain poorly understood, particularly in the regression setting with unbounded losses. Existing analyses rely on approximation or stability arguments and do not fully capture how physical structure influences generalisation from finite data. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for PIML that provides high-probability generalisation guarantees in the presence of unbounded losses. We adopt a multi-task perspective that jointly treats data fidelity, PDE residuals, initial and boundary conditions, avoiding the looseness induced by standard union-bound approaches. Our analysis leverages the structure of physics-informed objectives to derive novel bounds where the complexity scales with input-gradient norms of the losses, revealing a direct link between physical regularity and generalisation. We instantiate this framework under Sobolev and Poincaré-type assumptions, yielding two classes of bounds that trade off statistical complexity and smoothness in different regimes. Building on these results, we propose a self-bounding-aware learning algorithm that directly optimises tractable surrogates of the derived bounds, along with a practical procedure to estimate the associated constants in realistic settings. Empirical evaluations on standard PDE benchmarks demonstrate that our bounds are non-vacuous, significantly tighter than union-bound baselines, and can be effectively minimised during training. Overall, our results provide a principled statistical foundation for the generalisation of physics-informed models.

</details>

Conclusion: 该方法能有效检测语义约束违反型异常，同时输出标量异常分数和规则级归因，且无需真实异常图像即可训练。

Abstract: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs, but violations of semantic constraints: objects co-occur in structured ways, actions imply preconditions, and events satisfy temporal or relational regularities. We study anomaly detection in this setting, where constraints are given as logical rules over learned visual concepts, but real rule violations are rare or absent during training. We propose a neural rule evaluator that compiles each constraint into a directed acyclic graph and learns feature-aware subtree MLP gates for its internal logical operators. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to a parent representation and a rule-satisfaction probability, with intermediate supervision obtained from exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. The key difficulty is that same-image training data often provide insufficient coverage of informative truth configurations and also allow shortcut solutions. To address this, we introduce chimera training: an operand-level counterfactual construction at the feature level. Instead of mixing input images, we concatenate subtree features from different samples; each operand keeps the hard truth label of the sample it came from, and the chimera target is obtained by applying the node's logical operator to those inherited labels. This supplies supervised logical counterexamples without requiring real anomalous images. Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR, the resulting evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules. The method yields both scalar anomaly scores and rule-level attributions.

</details>


### [38] [ARBITER: Reasoning Trajectory Basins and Majority Vote Failures in Test-Time Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26172)
*Meng Cai,Lars Kulik,Farhana Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现语言模型测试时采样的多个推理轨迹会聚集成少数几个"推理盆地"，多数投票会选择最稳定的盆地而非最准确的盆地，导致正确答案被否决的错误。作者提出ARBITER方法，通过分析盆地间相互作用来修正这种错误，在不借助外部信息的情况下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示语言模型测试时采样中多数投票的固有缺陷：推理轨迹并非独立，而是聚集成少数几个"推理盆地"，多数投票选择的是最稳定而非最准确的盆地，导致正确答案虽存在但被否决的"错误多数"失败模式。

Method: 提出ARBITER框架，包含两种变体：(1) ARBITER-Δ在多数先验上添加同模型证据；(2) ARBITER-Enc利用完整解决方案的隐藏状态中的有界残差信号。该方法仅使用基础模型自身的采样输出、隐藏状态和派生证据，无需外部信息。

Result: 在GSM8K上，Qwen3-4B的K=24采样共识达94%左右，同池top-2 oracle约96%左右。ARBITER恢复部分oracle差距。在三个模型家族和三个数学基准上持续改进，无净负案例。如Llama-3.1-8B的MMLU-HS-Math从78%提升至82%，恢复约22%的oracle空间。

Conclusion: 测试时采样的推理轨迹存在聚类现象，多数投票存在系统性偏差，而ARBITER能够从样本池自身部分恢复这种性能差距，验证了该头room可被部分回收。

Abstract: When language models use test-time sampling, they generate multiple reasoning trajectories and select an answer by majority vote. We show that these trajectories are not independent: for a given question, they concentrate into a small number of clusters, or reasoning basins, each defined by a normalized final answer and the solutions that reach it. A majority vote therefore selects the most stable basin rather than the most accurate one, which creates wrong-majority failures where the correct answer is present but outvoted. We introduce ARBITER, a model-agnostic approach that models interactions between basins using only the base model's own sampled outputs, hidden states, and derived evidence. Most direct correction strategies fail; ARBITER instead uses conservative additive evidence on top of consensus. In its simplest parameter-free form, ARBITER-Δ adds same-model evidence to the majority prior, while ARBITER-Enc augments this with bounded residual signals from hidden states over complete solutions. On GSM8K with Qwen3-4B, consensus over K=24 samples achieves around the mid-94% range, while a same-pool top-2 oracle reaches around the mid-96% range. ARBITER recovers a subset of these cases using zero external information. Across three model families and three math benchmarks, it yields consistent gains with no net-negative cases; for example, on Llama-3.1-8B MMLU-HS-Math, it improves accuracy from the mid-78% range to the mid-82% range, recovering about 22% of the available oracle headroom, indicating that this headroom can be partially recovered from the sample pool itself.

</details>


### [39] [Online Learning on Hidden-Convex Losses via Algorithmic Equivalence: Optimal Regret, Geometric Barrier, and Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26373)
*Anas Barakat,Andreas Kontogiannis,Vasilis Pollatos,Ioannis Panageas,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在隐藏凸损失(经非线性重新参数化后变为凸的非凸损失)的对抗性在线学习中，在线梯度下降(OGD)可达到最优的O(√T)遗憾界，解决了Ghai等人(2022)的公开问题。此外，用Hessian相容性条件取代对角雅可比条件，扩大了可容许重新参数化的类别，并给出了下界证明该条件的必要性。在单点bandit反馈设置下，OGD达到O(T^{3/4})遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022)证明了隐藏凸损失下OGD可模拟在线镜像下降(OMD)并达到O(T^{2/3})遗憾，但他们留下了一个公开问题：能否恢复在线凸优化中最优的Θ(√T)遗憾界。本文旨在肯定地回答这一问题，并澄清算法等价所需的几何条件。

Method: 通过更锐利的离散时间算法等价论证，证明OGD在相同假设下达到O(√T)遗憾；用Hessian相容性条件（必要且充分）替换对角雅可比充分条件；构造反例证明条件不成立时OGD遭受Ω(T)遗憾；将分析扩展至单点bandit反馈。

Result: OGD在隐藏凸损失下达到O(√T)遗憾（匹配最优最坏情况率）；Hessian相容性条件是算法等价的必要且充分条件；条件不满足时OGD遭受Ω(T)遗憾；bandit OGD达到O(T^{3/4})期望遗憾。

Conclusion: 隐藏凸设置下可恢复在线凸优化的最优遗憾率；Hessian相容性条件刻画了算法等价所需的精确几何；结果揭示了非凸优化与凸优化之间更深的联系。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning with hidden-convex losses, i.e., nonconvex losses that become convex after a nonlinear reparameterization. Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) proved that, under geometric and smoothness assumptions, online gradient descent (OGD) on such nonconvex losses approximately simulates online mirror descent (OMD) on the underlying convex losses with a suitable regularizer, yielding $\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret. They left open whether the optimal $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret from online convex optimization can be recovered in this hidden-convex setting. We answer this question affirmatively. More specifically, via a sharper discrete-time algorithmic equivalence argument, we prove that OGD achieves $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under the same assumptions, matching the optimal worst-case rate for adversarial online convex optimization. We also address another open question of Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) by clarifying the geometry required for this algorithmic equivalence. We replace the diagonal-Jacobian sufficient condition with a necessary-and-sufficient Hessian compatibility condition, thereby expanding the class of admissible reparameterizations. We complement our tight regret bound with a lower bound showing that the Hessian compatibility assumption is essential for OGD; when it fails, we construct a smooth reparameterization and an adversarial sequence of hidden-convex losses for which OGD suffers $Ω(T)$ regret. Finally, we extend our analysis to one-point bandit feedback and prove a $\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$ expected regret bound for bandit OGD with spherical smoothing, matching its classical rate on convex losses.

</details>


### [40] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InfoQuant，一种无需训练的低比特激活量化方法，通过峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）将激活分布重塑为更利于量化的形式，在W4A4KV4设置下平均保留97%的浮点精度，将LLaMA-2 13B的性能差距较先前最优方法缩小42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活量化是大语言模型高效部署的主要瓶颈，现有后训练量化方法尽管抑制了异常值、平衡了通道或最小化重建误差，但很少明确什么样的激活分布才是易于离散化的，导致激活值看似平滑却仍存在大量化误差。

Method: 从信息论角度分析量化误差，指出量化友好的激活应同时具有较小的数值范围和该范围内充分的分散性。据此提出InfoQuant，一种无需训练的方法，采用峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）将激活重塑为更利于量化的分布，并引入自适应异常值token选择提升优化鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个LLM家族上，InfoQuant一致优于先前的PTQ和端到端训练基线。在W4A4KV4设定下，平均保留97%的浮点精度，将LLaMA-2 13B的性能差距较先前最优方法缩小42%。

Conclusion: 通过从信息论角度重新审视激活量化问题，明确量化友好分布的设计准则，InfoQuant实现了无需训练的低比特激活量化性能突破，有效缩小了量化模型与浮点模型的差距。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [41] [Function-Valued Causal Influence in Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26408)
*Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在基于非线性机器学习模型的时间序列因果发现中，使用标量边分数会严重丢失信息，并提出用函数值因果影响（functional-valued causal influence）来替代，通过个体条件期望（ICE）框架从训练好的模型中直接估计因果响应函数，实验表明标量分数无法区分单调、阈值、饱和及符号翻转等不同功能行为，民主发展案例进一步揭示了分数中心方法遗漏的体制特异性因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列因果发现方法在使用非线性模型时，几乎总是将因果关联总结为单一的标量边分数。作者认为这种做法严重丢失信息，因为非线性自回归模型学习到的是状态依赖函数，其效应会随机制、幅度和上下文而变化，标量分数无法捕捉这种复杂性。

Method: 以神经加性向量自回归（Neural Additive Vector Autoregression）为代表架构，提出基于个体条件期望（Individual Conditional Expectation, ICE）的实用框架，直接从训练好的模型中估计因果响应函数，从而实现函数值因果影响的量化。

Result: 通过受控合成实验证明，标量分数无法区分的边可能表现出截然不同的功能行为（单调、阈值、饱和、符号翻转）。民主发展案例研究发现，函数值分析能够揭示分数中心方法系统性遗漏的体制特异性和不对称因果结构。

Conclusion: 标量因果分数是非线性时序因果发现中的严重信息瓶颈，函数值因果影响能更完整地捕捉非线性模型的真实学习结果，建议未来研究在使用非线性模型时采用函数值视角替代标量汇总方式。

Abstract: Causal discovery in time series is increasingly performed using nonlinear machine-learning models, yet the resulting causal relationships are almost always summarized by scalar edge scores. We argue that this practice obscures the true object learned by nonlinear autoregressive models: a state-dependent function whose effect varies across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. We formalize function-valued causal influence for additive, contribution-decomposable architectures and show that scalar causal scores constitute a severe information bottleneck, conflating between-state variation with within-state residual noise. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a representative architecture, we introduce a practical framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation for estimating causal response functions directly from trained models. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors, including monotonic, thresholded, saturating, and sign-changing effects. An applied case study on democratic development further shows that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

</details>


### [42] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAC提出了一种噪声感知的自适应混合比例控制器，通过在线估计梯度方差和信号分歧来动态调整监督微调与强化学习的混合权重，在数学、编程、科学和逻辑等基准测试中优于固定和基于规则的混合策略，且训练开销低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合后训练通常结合监督微调和强化学习，但固定的混合调度无法适应两种信号相对噪声随时间变化的情况，需要一种自适应混合机制。

Method: 提出GAC（噪声感知控制器），从梯度方差和两种训练信号之间的分歧的在线估计中推导出自适应混合权重，并加入平滑、先验引导和有界更新，同时复用已有的训练张量。

Result: 在数学、编程、科学和逻辑基准测试中，GAC在强固定基线和基于规则的基线上持续改进混合后训练，在更大模型规模上提升更显著，训练开销低于1%。

Conclusion: GAC作为一种轻量级的自适应混合策略，能够有效提升混合后训练的性能，尤其在更大模型上优势更明显。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于Transformer的表格基础模型TabPFN在儿童贫血预测中的表现，发现其在低数据场景下优于传统机器学习模型，但模型选择对性能的影响小于人群异质性。
