A central difficulty is that cross-frequency transfer is inherently asymmetric: coarse amplitude structure remains relatively stable across frequencies, whereas phase-sensitive oscillatory structure deteriorates much more rapidly as frequency increases. Motivated by this asymmetry, we propose APEX, Amplitude-anchored and Phase-prior-guided Enhancement from eXtrapolated coarse predictions, a framework for target-scarce higher-frequency wave-field prediction. A lower-frequency neural operator first provides a coarse prediction in the target-frequency regime, from which we retain only the amplitude as a transferable structural anchor. A conditional flow-matching enhancer then reconstructs the target higher-frequency field under the guidance of a Green's-function-inspired phase prior. Experiments on SimpleWave, Helmholtz, and Maxwell benchmarks show that APEX consistently outperforms direct lower-to-higher extrapolation, target-adapted operator, and joint generative baselines under limited target-frequency supervision. Our results suggest that reliable higher-frequency prediction of oscillatory wave fields should not rely on direct end-to-end transfer of the full complex field, but instead on explicitly reusing transferable coarse structure while separately recovering the missing oscillatory detail.

</details>


### [119] [Stabilizing Recurrent Dynamics for Test-Time Scalable Latent Reasoning in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26733)
*Xiao-Wen Yang,Ziyu Han,Xi-Hua Zhang,Wen-Da Wei,Jie-Jing Shao,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STARS通过雅可比谱半径正则化约束潜状态收敛到渐近稳定不动点，解决了Looped Language Models在深度循环推理中性能先升后降的不稳定问题，在算术和复杂数学推理任务上实现了可靠的测试时缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Language Models在深度循环推理中表现出不可靠的测试时缩放行为，性能在特定迭代深度达到峰值后随循环加深而崩溃，存在稳定性与有效性之间的固有权衡。

Method: 提出STARS训练框架，通过高效雅可比谱半径正则化结合随机循环采样，约束潜状态收敛到渐近稳定不动点，在确保严格稳定性的同时最大化有效性。

Result: 在算术任务上实现可靠的测试时缩放，在复杂数学推理任务上显著缓解了随循环深度增加的性能退化，同时提升了峰值性能。

Conclusion: 将推理概念化为不确定性降低，向稳定不动点收敛是解决循环推理中稳定性与有效性权衡的有效途径。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) enable efficient latent reasoning through depth recurrence, yet exhibit unreliable test-time scaling behavior: performance often peaks at a certain iteration depth and then collapses with further recurrence. Through latent dynamics analysis, we find an inherent trade-off between stability and effectiveness in existing architectures and strategies. By conceptualizing reasoning as uncertainty reduction, we propose that convergence toward stable fixed points while preserving effectiveness represents a promising way. To this end, we propose STARS (STAbility-driven Recurrent Scaling), a training framework that constrains latent states to approach asymptotically stable fixed points. This is realized via efficient Jacobian Spectral Radius Regularization with random loop sampling, enabling STARS to maximize effectiveness while ensuring rigorous stability. Experiments on arithmetic tasks show that STARS achieves reliable test-time scaling, and on complex mathematical reasoning it substantially mitigates performance degradation as recurrence depth increases while also improving peak performance.

</details>


### [120] [Localizing Memorized Regions in Diffusion Models via Coordinate-Wise Curvature Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26756)
*Gwangho Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何特征（坐标方差坍塌）的局部记忆检测方法，通过曲率差分技术区分过拟合导致的记忆与数据固有约束，并在Stable Diffusion上取得了优于先前方法的定位效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆检测方法多依赖全局或模型级信号，无法定位记忆在生成图像中的具体区域，且难以区分过拟合导致的记忆与数据固有约束。

Method: 将局部记忆表征为坐标方差的几何坍塌；提出曲率差分方法（减去欠拟合基线的曲率）以隔离过拟合驱动的记忆；推导出分数差分代理，为广泛使用的基于分数差分的检测指标提供几何解释。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion上，以真实记忆掩码为基准，该方法在记忆定位任务上优于先前的基于注意力的定位方法。

Conclusion: 曲率差分方法能有效区分过拟合记忆与数据固有约束，且分数差分代理提供了统一的几何解释框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models can unintentionally memorize training samples, raising concerns about privacy and copyright. While recent methods can detect memorization, they often rely on global or model-specific signals and provide limited insight into where memorization appears within a generated image. We provide a geometric characterization of local memorization as a coordinate-wise variance collapse. However, such collapse can also arise from intrinsic data constraints rather than overfitting. To isolate overfitting-driven memorization, we propose curvature-difference methods that subtract the curvature of an underfitted baseline, either the unconditional model or a less-trained version of itself. We further derive a score-difference proxy that provides a geometric explanation for the widely used score-difference-based detection metric. Experiments on Stable Diffusion, evaluated against ground-truth memorization masks, show that our method outperforms the prior attention-based localization method. Code is available at https://github.com/Gwangho99/mem-curv-diff.

</details>


### [121] [Time Series Causal Discovery via Context-Conditioned and Causality-Augmented Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26759)
*Biao Ouyang,Tengxue Zhang,Zhihao Zhuang,Yang Shu,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PTCD是一个面向时间序列因果发现的预训练框架，通过上下文条件建模和可迁移因果增强技术，提升跨任务泛化能力，在多个现实世界OOD数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果发现方法依赖特定数据集的优化，难以将因果发现能力迁移到具有不同因果机制的新时间序列上。

Method: 1) 双尺度迭代注意力机制捕获窗口级因果关系；2) 高斯混合与上下文级路由机制处理异质外生分布；3) 基于合成数据集的预训练范式，结合干预学习和因果混合策略，促进稳定因果发现和强泛化。

Result: 在多个真实世界OOD数据集上进行的大量实验表明，PTCD在因果发现和根因识别方面均表现出色。

Conclusion: PTCD通过预训练框架有效提升了时间序列因果发现的跨任务泛化能力，适用于追踪异常根因等实际应用场景。

Abstract: Causal discovery from time series is critical for many real-world applications, such as tracing the root causes of anomalies. Existing approaches typically rely on dataset-specific optimization, making it difficult to transfer their causal discovery capabilities to new time series governed by diverse causal mechanisms. In this paper, we propose \textbf{PTCD}, a novel \textbf{P}retraining framework for \textbf{T}ime-series \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{D}iscovery, which improves cross-task generalization through context-conditioned modeling and transferable causal augmentation. To model complex temporal causal dependencies, PTCD employs a dual-scale iterative attention mechanism to capture window-level causal relationships, and a Gaussian mixture with a context-level routing mechanism to handle heterogeneous exogenous distributions. To further address distribution shifts across causal graphs, PTCD adopts a pretraining paradigm on synthetic datasets that integrates intervention-based learning and a causal mixup strategy, promoting stable causal discovery and stronger generalization. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets demonstrate that PTCD excels in both causal discovery and root cause identification.

</details>


### [122] [Adversarial Training for Robust Coverage Network under Worst-case Facility Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26763)
*Changhao Miao,Yuntian Zhang,Tongyu Wu,Fang Deng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对抗学习的双智能体深度强化学习（DADRL）框架来解决最大覆盖设施选址-中断问题（MCLIP），该框架包含一个选址智能体和一个中断智能体，分别对应双层优化中的上层和下层，通过动态对抗训练和基于代理的集成推理策略，在合成和真实数据集上实现了优越的计算效率和竞争力强的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCLIP是一类经典的双层优化问题，对韧性基础设施规划至关重要，但由于上下层之间的强耦合以及各自的高组合复杂性，传统方法难以有效求解。

Method: 提出双智能体深度强化学习（DADRL）框架：1）选址智能体与不断进化的中断智能体同步对抗训练，捕捉上下层间的动态竞争关系；2）提出基于代理的集成推理策略，利用训练好的中断智能体作为高保真代理来指导选址智能体的决策。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法相比其他基线在保持高度竞争力解质量的同时，实现了优越的计算效率。

Conclusion: DADRL框架对网络结构具有模型无关性，其对抗学习范式在解决其他双层优化问题上展现出强大潜力。

Abstract: The Maximal Covering Location-Interdiction Problem (MCLIP) is a classic bi-level optimization problem, which is fundamental to resilient infrastructure planning yet remains computationally intractable. Specifically, the upper level determines facility locations to maximize coverage, while the lower level executes worst-case interdiction to minimize the coverage. The strong coupling between the upper and lower levels, combined with their respective high combinatorial complexity, renders traditional methods ineffective. To bridge this gap, we propose a Dual-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning (DADRL) framework based on adversarial learning, comprising a location agent corresponding to the upper level and an interdiction agent corresponding to the lower level. Our contributions are threefold: (1) The location agent is trained simultaneously against an evolving interdiction agent, making it effectively capture the dynamic competitive interplay between the upper and lower levels; (2) To fully exploit the learned capabilities of the interdiction agent, we propose a Surrogate-based Ensemble Inference Strategy that utilizes the trained interdiction agent as a high-fidelity surrogate to guide the decisions of location agent; (3) Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior computational efficiency while maintaining highly competitive solution quality compared to other baselines. Furthermore, our DADRL framework is model-agnostic to network structures, while its underlying adversarial learning paradigm demonstrates strong potential for solving other bi-level optimization problems.

</details>


### [123] [Towards Generalization-Oriented Models for Vehicle Routing Problems with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26776)
*Changhao Miao,Yuntian Zhang,Tongyu Wu,Fang Deng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R2E-IG框架，通过残差精炼专家网络、实例级门控机制和混合分布训练（含动态权重自适应），提升深度强化学习方法在车辆路径问题上的跨分布泛化能力，在分布内和分布外实例上均取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DRL-based VRP方法通常在均匀分布生成的数据上训练，导致在真实世界的分布偏移下性能受限，亟需一种能够跨分布泛化的模型。

Method: (1) 残差精炼专家(R2E)架构，通过残差精炼增强专家表达能力；(2) 实例级门控机制，学习分布感知的实例表示并将输入路由到合适的模块；(3) 混合分布训练机制及动态权重自适应(DWA)，动态重加权不同分布的训练数据以强调更具信息量的样本。

Result: 在合成数据和基准数据集上，R2E-IG在分布内和分布外实例上均达到与最先进基线可比的性能，且可轻松集成到现有DRL方法中进一步提升效果。

Conclusion: R2E-IG是一种通用且有效的跨分布泛化框架，能够显著提升DRL方法在VRP任务上的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In recent years, Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has achieved substantial progress on Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs). However, existing DRL-based methods are typically trained on instances generated from a uniform distribution, which limits their performance under real-world distribution shifts. In this paper, we aim to develop a generalization-oriented model that partitions the policy network into multiple modules and adaptively recombines modules to form specific policies during inference. Specifically, we propose Residual Refined Experts with Instance-level Gating (R2E-IG) to improve cross-distribution generalization. Our contributions are threefold: (1) We introduce a Residual Refined Expert (R2E) architecture that enhance expert expressiveness via residual refinement; (2) We design an instance-level gating mechanism that learns distribution-aware instance representations and routes inputs to suitable modules; (3) We propose a mixed-distribution training mechanism equipped with Dynamic Weight Adaption (DWA), which dynamically reweights training data from different distributions to emphasize more informative ones. Extensive experiments show that R2E-IG achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art baselines on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution instances across synthetic and benchmark datasets. Moreover, R2E-IG is generic and can be easily integrated into existing DRL-based methods to further improve performance.

</details>


### [124] [Ratio-Variance Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26784)
*Yu Luo,Shuo Han,Yihan Hu,Lei Lv,Huaping Liu,Fuchun Sun,Jianye Hao,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R²VPO算法，通过约束策略比率方差来替代PPO中的硬裁剪，实现原则性的信任区域约束。该方法在7种LLM规模、10种机器人控制任务上优于PPO基线，显著提升了数学推理能力和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准on-policy强化学习依赖启发式裁剪来强制执行信任区域，但这种机制会不加区分地截断高回报但高散度的更新，限制了模型从新颖探索中学习的能力。作者希望找到一种更原则性的替代方案。

Method: 提出R²VPO（Ratio-Variance Regularized Policy Optimization），通过约束策略比率（importance ratio）的方差来近似信任区域约束，采用原始-对偶优化框架实现。这种方法充当分布式的"软刹车"，保留高回报更新的梯度信号，同时自然降低陈旧off-policy数据的权重并允许其复用。

Result: 在7种LLM规模（涵盖快速和慢速推理范式）和10种机器人控制任务上进行评估。在数学推理基准上取得显著性能提升，小模型改进尤为突出；在连续控制领域（特别是稀疏奖励和动态环境）中一致优于PPO基线，样本效率显著提升。

Conclusion: 比率方差正则化可以作为一种原则性的基础，用于稳定且数据高效的政策优化，替代传统PPO中的硬裁剪机制。

Abstract: Standard on-policy reinforcement learning relies on heuristic clipping to enforce trust regions, but this mechanism imposes a severe cost by indiscriminately truncating high-return yet high-divergence updates. We demonstrate that explicitly constraining the policy ratio variance provides a principled local approximation to trust-region constraints, eliminating the need for binary hard clipping. By acting as a distributional ``soft brake'', this approach preserves critical gradient signals from novel discoveries while naturally down-weighting and enabling the reuse of stale, off-policy data. We introduce ${\bf R}^2{\bf VPO}$ (Ratio-Variance Regularized Policy Optimization), which implements this constraint via a primal-dual optimization framework. Extensive evaluations across $7$ LLM scales, spanning both fast and slow reasoning paradigms, and $10$ robotic control tasks demonstrate the generality of the proposed approach. R$^2$VPO achieves substantial performance gains on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with particularly pronounced improvements on smaller models, while significantly improving sample efficiency. Furthermore, it consistently outperforms PPO baselines in continuous control domains, particularly in sparse-reward and dynamic environments. Together, these findings establish ratio-variance regularization as a principled foundation for stable and data-efficient policy optimization.

</details>


### [125] [Pretrained Approximators for Low-Thrust Trajectory Cost and Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26790)
*Zhong Zhang,Giacomo Acciarini,Dario Izzo,Hexi Baoyin,Francesco Topputo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用机器学习代理模型替代传统最优控制求解，实现低推力轨迹设计的快速燃料消耗和转移可行性评估。通过构建大规模数据集和同伦-射线策略，引入自相似变换实现跨轨道参数和中心天体的泛化，并验证了缩放定律的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低推力轨迹设计依赖大量昂贵的燃料消耗和转移可行性最优控制求解，计算成本过高，亟需高效的替代方法以支撑大规模场景评估。

Method: 构建机器学习代理模型；提出同伦-射线策略生成大规模数据集；引入自相似变换实现跨半长轴、倾角和中心天体的泛化；利用缩放定律指导模型容量和数据集规模设计。

Result: 模型能精确预测最优燃料消耗和最小转移时间；性能随训练数据和网络参数的对数线性提升，未出现饱和；在公开数据集、GTOC多小行星飞越问题和交会任务中验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 机器学习代理可有效替代低推力轨迹优化中的最优控制求解，缩放定律指导下的模型在多种轨道环境和任务类别中表现良好，开源发布以支持空间社区。

Abstract: Low-thrust trajectory design relies heavily on repeated evaluations of fuel consumption and transfer feasibility, which require expensive optimal control solutions. In this work, we show these quantities can be accurately approximated by machine learning surrogates, enabling fast and scalable evaluation across a wide range of scenarios. By increasing both dataset size and model capacity, we observe that low-thrust trajectory optimization follows a scaling law, with performance improving linearly with the logarithm of training data and network parameters, and no evidence of saturation within the explored regime. Guided by this observation, we construct a large-scale dataset using the proposed homotopy-ray strategy tailored to mission design requirements. A key is the introduction of a self-similar transformation, which allows generalization across semi-major axes, inclinations, and central bodies avoiding retraining. As a result, the same neural approximator can be applied to diverse orbital environments and mission classes. The proposed models accurately predict optimal fuel consumption and minimum transfer time for single- and multi-revolution transfers. Their performance and generalization are demonstrated on a public dataset, a multi-asteroid flyby problem from the Global Trajectory Optimization Competition, and an asteroid rendezvous mission design. The models and datasets are released as open-source to support the space community.

</details>


### [126] [Latent Recurrent Transformer: Architecture Exploration, Training Strategies, and Scaling Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26797)
*Zeyi Huang,Xuehai He,LiLiang Ren,Yiping Wang,Baolin Peng,Hao Cheng,Shuohang Wang,Pengcheng He,Jianfeng Gao,Yong Jae Lee,Yelong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LRT（潜在循环Transformer）通过对自回归Transformer进行轻量级增强，复用前一个token的高层源隐藏状态作为当前token的循环记忆，在不增加暂停token或额外深度循环的情况下实现跨位置的循环潜在路径。通过交错并行训练方法，在约2倍基线计算量下实现循环记忆感知的预训练，仅增加0.3%参数即可改善语言建模损失和上下文学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持标准注意力机制和KV-cache接口不变的前提下，以极小的参数开销为自回归Transformer引入循环记忆能力，从而在不显著增加计算成本的情况下提升语言建模和上下文学习性能。

Method: LRT复用前一个token的高层源隐藏状态作为当前token的循环记忆，通过跨层的循环潜在路径连接不同位置。训练采用交错并行训练方法：先进行一次全序列初始化前向传播构建共享缓冲区，然后并行优化不相交的位置子集并写回，使得所有token都能在约2倍基线计算量下获得循环记忆感知的监督信号。

Result: 在nanochat风格骨干网络和多种tokens-per-parameter预算下，LRT在匹配有效计算量的条件下同时改善了语言建模损失和上下文学习能力，仅增加约0.3%的参数。

Conclusion: LRT以极小的参数和计算开销成功为自回归Transformer引入了有效的循环记忆机制，在不改变标准注意力架构和KV-cache接口的前提下，提升了语言建模和上下文学习性能。

Abstract: We study Latent Recurrent Transformer (LRT), a lightweight augmentation of autoregressive transformers that reuses a high-level source-layer hidden state from the previous token as recurrent memory for the next token. Because this source state is already computed during ordinary decoding, LRT adds a cross-layer recurrent latent pathway across positions without inserting pause tokens or extra depth loops, and the standard attention mechanism and KV-cache interface are preserved. To pretrain this recurrence at scale without sequentially unrolling the transformer, we introduce interleaved parallel training: a single full-sequence initialization forward pass builds a shared buffer; then disjoint position subsets are refined in parallel and written back, so that all tokens receive recurrent-memory-aware supervision at roughly 2 times baseline compute. Across nanochat style backbones and a wide range of tokens-per-parameter budgets, LRT improves both language-modeling loss and in-context learning under matched effective compute while adding as little as 0.3% parameters.

</details>


### [127] [PATE-TabTransGAN: Differentially Private Synthetic Tabular Data Generation via Transformer-Based Student Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26802)
*M. Youssef,M. Woźniak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PATE-TabTransGAN框架，结合PATE机制与Transformer学生判别器，在形式化差分隐私保护下生成高质量表格数据。实验在四个基准数据集上达到最优或持平的AUROC，并分析了AUCPR指标对正类约定的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私表格数据生成方法难以兼顾强理论保护与高保真合成：强隐私保护方法牺牲特征依赖建模能力，而能捕捉复杂列关系的架构仅提供经验性隐私保证。

Method: 集成Logistic回归教师模型在不相交数据分区上训练，通过噪声聚合标签监督Transformer学生判别器；残差生成器针对该差分隐私学生进行优化，通过后处理继承形式化(ε,δ)-DP保证；采用GNMax RDP会计机制实现数值稳定的隐私核算。

Result: 在Adult、Breast、Cardio、Cervical四个数据集上，PATE-TabTransGAN在所有数据集上达到最优或持平AUROC；在AUCPR指标上，Cardio持平最优基线，Cervical领先，Breast落后；Adult上的AUCPR差距源于评价管线的正类约定差异而非合成质量缺陷。

Conclusion: PATE-TabTransGAN通过结合PATE机制与Transformer架构，有效弥合了形式化差分隐私与高质量表格数据合成之间的鸿沟，为隐私保护表格生成提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity synthetic tabular data under formal differential privacy guarantees remains an open challenge. Methods that provide strong theoretical protection typically sacrifice the modeling of inter-feature dependencies required for realistic synthesis, while architectures that excel at capturing complex column relationships offer only empirical privacy guarantees. We present PATE-TabTransGAN, a generative framework that integrates the Private Aggregation of Teacher Ensembles (PATE) mechanism with a Transformer-based student discriminator to jointly address both requirements, and employs a GNMax RDP accountant for numerically stable privacy accounting. An ensemble of Logistic Regression teachers trained on disjoint partitions supervise the student via noisy-aggregated labels, and a residual generator is optimized against this differentially private student, inheriting formal (ε, δ)-DP guarantees by post-processing. PATE-TabTransGAN was compared with PATE-GAN, DP-GAN, and DP-CTGAN, considered state-of-the-art in differentially private tabular synthesis. Experiments conducted on four tabular benchmarks (Adult, Breast, Cardio, Cervical) confirmed the high quality of the proposed method: PATE-TabTransGAN attains the best or tied-best AUROC on all four datasets. On AUCPR it matches the strongest baseline on Cardio, leads on Cervical, and trails on Breast; on Adult, we demonstrate that AUCPR is highly sensitive to positive-class convention, and that the observed gap is consistent with a convention difference between evaluation pipelines rather than a synthesis deficit.

</details>


### [128] [Innovation: An Almost Characterization of Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26808)
*Nishant P. Das,Piyush Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"创新"（innovation）概念来描述LLM生成训练数据之外内容的倾向，证明其与幻觉几乎等价，并基于创新率与缺失质量的关系，给出了新的幻觉率下界，扩展了Kalai和Vempala的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答两个基本问题：(i) 校准的LLM为何不可避免地产生幻觉？(ii) 放弃校准是否能避免幻觉？通过引入"创新"属性来刻画幻觉的根源。

Method: 提出"创新"（innovation）概念，证明其在概率框架下与幻觉的几乎等价关系：幻觉蕴含创新，创新以高概率蕴含幻觉。同时建立创新率与缺失质量（missing mass）的联系，推导新幻觉率下界。

Result: 证明了创新是幻觉的几乎充要条件；基于创新率给出了幻觉率的下界；通过与缺失质量的关联，扩展了Kalai和Vempala关于幻觉率的结论。

Conclusion: 校准的LLM因"创新"这一内在属性而不可避免地产生幻觉，放弃校准也无法从根本上避免幻觉。创新率是理解幻觉行为的关键量化指标。

Abstract: Hallucination is a central limitation of large language models (LLMs), and substantial effort has been devoted to understanding and mitigating it. Towards this, Kalai and Vempala (STOC 2024) introduced a probabilistic framework formalizing calibration and hallucination, and showed that, with high probability, calibrated LLMs hallucinate roughly at the rate of the "missing mass", a measure of how incomplete the training data is relative to its source. This raises two fundamental questions: (i) what property of a calibrated LLM makes hallucinations unavoidable? and (ii) can hallucinations be avoided by giving up calibration? We answer these questions by introducing a simpler property we call innovation that measures the tendency of a model to produce outputs outside the training data. We show that innovation is implied by the condition for hallucination identified by Kalai and Vempala, and, further, that it is an almost characterization of hallucination: hallucination implies innovation, and conversely, innovation implies hallucination with high probability. We also provide lower bounds on the hallucination rate based on the "innovation rate", and by relating innovation rate back to missing mass, we obtain new hallucination rate lower bounds based on missing mass that extend the results of Kalai and Vempala.

</details>


### [129] [The Kalman Evolve: Closing the Gap in Kalman Filtering via Interpretable Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26830)
*Vasileios Saketos,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kalman Evolve框架，利用大语言模型联合优化卡尔曼滤波器的噪声参数和更新结构，通过生成可解释的非仿射修改，在多个跟踪基准上实现高达12%的RMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波器在线性高斯假设下最优，但实际传感场景（如多普勒雷达、LiDAR）中噪声非高斯、动力学非线性，导致仿射估计器存在固有性能瓶颈，仅调参无法消除这一差距。

Method: 提出Kalman Evolve框架，利用大语言模型作为程序空间的先验，联合优化噪声协方差参数与滤波器更新结构，生成保持递归形式的可解释非仿射修改。

Result: 在合成和真实跟踪基准（多普勒雷达、LiDAR定位、行人跟踪）上，发现算法相比优化后的卡尔曼滤波器基线，RMSE降低高达12%。

Conclusion: 优化卡尔曼滤波器的结构而非仅优化参数，是提升状态估计性能的实用且可解释方法。

Abstract: State estimation is a fundamental problem in control and signal processing, for which the Kalman Filter provides an optimal solution under linear dynamics, Gaussian noise, and known noise covariances. However, these assumptions often fail in realistic sensing settings such as Doppler radar and LiDAR. In these cases, the optimal estimator is inherently nonlinear, which leads to systematic performance degradation. This creates a performance gap that cannot be eliminated by tuning the noise covariance parameters (i.e., the process and measurement noise in the Kalman Filter) alone. To address this limitation, we propose Kalman Evolve, a framework for discovering improved filtering algorithms by jointly optimizing both noise parameters and the update structure. Our approach leverages large language models (LLMs) as a structured prior over program space, enabling the generation of interpretable, non-affine modifications to the classical Kalman filter while preserving its recursive form. We provide analytical results establishing the suboptimality of affine estimators under common nonlinear sensing models, motivating the need for structure-aware updates. Across a range of synthetic and real-world tracking benchmarks, including Doppler radar, LiDAR-based localization, and pedestrian tracking, the discovered algorithms consistently improve over strong baselines such as the Optimized Kalman Filter, achieving up to 12\% reduction in RMSE. These results suggest that optimizing the structure of the Kalman filter, rather than only its parameters, provides a practical and interpretable way to improve state estimation.

</details>


### [130] [Periodic Topological Deep Learning for Polymer Design and Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26833)
*Yasharth Yadav,Tze Kwang Gerald Er,Atsushi Goto,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Periodic-TDL，一种基于周期Vietoris-Rips复合体与层次单纯复形消息传递（HSMP）的深度学习框架，能够捕捉聚合物中多体相互作用与周期性结构。该模型在电子、光学、物理和热学性质预测任务上全面超越现有方法，并通过对48,208个结构的计算数据集以及6对独立实验合成的聚合物进行验证，定量证实了酯到酰胺取代和α-甲基化可显著提升热稳定性（Tg分别升高约55°C和14°C）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的高分子性质预测方法通常将聚合物表示为单一重复单元的分子图，忽略了聚合物链的周期性以及超越成对键的多体相互作用，导致无法准确捕捉高分子的结构-性质关系。

Method: 构建周期Vietoris-Rips复合体以捕捉多空间尺度上的多体相互作用，并设计层次单纯复形消息传递（HSMP）编码器，实现从长程相互作用到共价键的信息传播，从而生成富含高阶拓扑特征的表示。

Result: 在电子、光学、物理和热学性质预测任务上全面超越现有最先进模型；通过48,208个结构的计算数据集发现酯到酰胺取代使Tg升高约55°C，α-甲基化使Tg升高约14°C；6对独立实验合成的聚合物验证了模型预测。

Conclusion: Periodic-TDL不仅能在基准数据集上取得最佳预测性能，更能捕捉特定官能团修饰背后的物理效应，为聚合物材料的理性设计提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Polymers underpin applications across energy, healthcare, and materials science, yet their vast chemical space makes systematic discovery challenging. Most machine learning approaches represent polymers as molecular graphs of a single repeating unit, thereby missing both the periodicity of polymer chains and many-body interactions beyond pairwise bonds. We introduce Periodic-TDL, a deep learning framework built on periodic Vietoris-Rips complexes that capture many-body interactions across multiple spatial scales, followed by a hierarchical simplicial message-passing (HSMP) encoder that propagates information from long-range interactions to covalent bonds, yielding representations enriched by higher-order topological features. Periodic-TDL outperforms all state-of-the-art models across polymer property prediction tasks spanning electronic, optical, physical, and thermal targets. Furthermore, we quantitatively validate how ester-to-amide substitution and $α$-methylation enhance thermal stability. Using a computationally synthesized dataset of 48,208 structures-generated via systematic substitution of acrylate and acrylamide polymers-we observed a mean $T_g$ increase of $\sim 55^\circ$C for ester-to-amide substitutions and $\sim 14^\circ$C for backbone $α$-methylation across matched polymer pairs. To verify these predicted trends, we use our Periodic-TDL model to analyze six novel polymer pairs from independent experimental measurements, including three newly synthesized polymers previously unreported in the literature. The experimental data successfully confirmed the model's predictions. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that Periodic-TDL captures the underlying physical effects of specific functional group modifications, rather than merely optimizing predictive performance on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [131] [MONA: Muon Optimizer with Nesterov Acceleration for Scalable Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26842)
*Jiacheng Li,Jianchao Tan,Hongtao Xu,Jiaqi Zhang,Yifan Lu,Yerui Sun,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MONA 是一种结合 Muon 正交化框架与曲率感知加速的优化器，通过引入基于梯度差异指数滑动平均的加速项，帮助逃离尖锐局部极小值，在 1B 到 68B 参数的 MoE 预训练及下游任务上优于 Muon 和 AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器虽通过矩阵正交化提供了几何感知更新，但作为一阶方法仍会陷入尖锐局部极小值，需要引入曲率感知的加速机制来改善这一问题。

Method: 在 Muon 的梯度处理流程中直接添加一个加速项，该加速项通过梯度差异的指数滑动平均计算得到，使优化器既能保持 Muon 的谱范数正则化，又能逃离尖锐极小值。

Result: 在 1B 到 68B 三个规模的 MoE 预训练中，MONA 在收敛速度和下游任务性能上均优于 Muon 和 AdamW；在 68B-A3B 模型上的监督微调中，在通用能力、数学推理和代码生成基准上达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: MONA 通过桥接正交化框架与曲率感知加速，有效提升了大规模 MoE 模型训练的收敛质量和下游性能。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer has recently offered a promising alternative to AdamW for large language model training, leveraging matrix orthogonalization to produce geometry-aware updates. However, like all first-order methods, Muon can become trapped in sharp local minima. In this work, we present MONA, an optimizer that bridges Muon's orthogonalization framework with curvature-aware acceleration. MONA adds an acceleration term directly into Muon's gradient processing pipeline. This term is calculated from the exponential moving average of gradient differences. We provide a detailed convergence analysis for MONA, showing that the acceleration term enables escape from sharp minima while preserving Muon's spectral-norm regularization. Empirically, MONA achieves better convergence and downstream task performance compared to both Muon and AdamW across three scales of Mixture-of-Experts pretraining, spanning from 1B to 68B parameters, with the largest model trained on 1 trillion tokens. Furthermore, we conduct supervised fine-tuning on the MOE-68B-A3B model and evaluate it on general capability, mathematical reasoning, and code generation benchmarks, where MONA achieves SOTA performance.

</details>


### [132] [Not All Disagreement Is Learnable: Token Teachability in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26844)
*Yuanyi Wang,Su Lu,Yanggan Gu,Pengkai Wang,Yifan Yang,Zhaoyi Yan,Congkai Xie,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可教性感知的在线蒸馏（TA-OPD），通过选择教师信号中真正可学习的token位置进行蒸馏，仅用5%的token即可超越全token OPD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性OPD方法基于熵或KL散度非均匀性选择token，但原始KL分歧无法区分可学习分歧（教师将纠正质量分配给学生top-K候选）与不兼容分歧（教师质量落在学生当前支持集之外），导致选择信号粗粒度。

Method: 提出token可教性（teachability）概念，衡量教师信号与学生当前支持集的局部兼容性；基于此设计TA-OPD，一种轻量级token位置选择方法，仅对高可教性位置施加OPD损失，无需奖励模型或验证器。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Qwen3师生设置下，TA-OPD仅用5%保留token即可超越全token OPD，并优于基于熵和散度的基线方法。

Conclusion: 选择性OPD的本质应是选择可学习的教师信号，而非仅选择显著性token；可教性比原始KL能更好预测固定上下文改进。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts with token-level teacher supervision. Recent selective OPD methods exploit the non-uniformity of OPD signals by prioritizing high-entropy or high-disagreement tokens. We revisit this principle and ask: which token-level teacher signals are actually learnable? Using a fixed-context diagnostic that measures same-context teacher-student KL reduction, we show that raw KL disagreement is a coarse proxy for learning value. It conflates learnable disagreement, where the teacher assigns corrective mass to the student's top-K candidates, with incompatible disagreement, where the teacher places mass mostly off the student's current support. We formalize this local compatibility as token teachability and show that it better predicts fixed-context improvement than raw KL alone. Motivated by this finding, we propose Teachability-Aware OPD (TA-OPD), a lightweight token-position selection method that applies OPD loss to high-teachability positions without reward models or verifiers. Across Qwen2.5 and Qwen 3 teacher-student settings, TA-OPD often surpasses full-token OPD with only 5% retained tokens and improves over entropy- and divergence-based baselines. Our results reframe selective OPD as selecting learnable teacher signals rather than merely salient tokens.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Energy-Based Models from Stochastic Interpolants using Spatiotemporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26850)
*Hanlin Yu,RuiKang OuYang,Partha Kaushik,Arto Klami,Michael U. Gutmann,Omar Chehab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出stNCE框架，通过联合时空差异学习能量模型，统一了现有方法并在图像和分子密度估计上达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于随机插值的能量模型学习方法（如去噪分数匹配）存在两种不同的失败模式：仅依赖空间差异或仅依赖时间差异的方法各有缺陷，需要一种联合时空差异的学习框架来克服这些问题。

Method: 提出时空噪声对比估计（stNCE）框架，通过联合利用数据空间和时间两个维度的差异来学习能量模型，统一了多种现有训练目标，并衍生出新的训练目标。

Result: 在图像和分子数据集上的实验表明，stNCE的性能与最先进的密度估计方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: stNCE有效地解决了现有方法中空间或时间差异学习的失败模式，为能量模型训练提供了统一的框架和新的视角。

Abstract: Learning an energy-based model from data samples is a central problem in machine learning. Many recent and popular methods, such as denoising score matching for training energy-based diffusion models, use stochastic interpolants to corrupt data samples at different noise levels indexed by a time variable. This defines a joint density over both the data space and time, and most methods learn its energy through either spatial or temporal differences. We identify distinct failure modes for both of these approaches. To solve them, we propose Spatiotemporal Noise-Contrastive Estimation (stNCE), a framework for learning the energy through joint spatiotemporal differences. stNCE unifies many existing methods and leads to new training objectives. Experiments on images and molecules demonstrate performance competitive with state-of-the-art density estimation methods.

</details>


### [134] [RAPNet: Accelerating Algebraic Multigrid with Learned Sparse Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26854)
*Yali Fink,Ido Ben-Yair,Lars Ruthotto,Eran Treister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAPNet，一种图神经网络框架，通过学习直接从稀疏代数系统生成稀疏且鲁棒的粗网格算子，解决了代数多重网格中稀疏性与收敛质量之间的权衡问题，在多种PDE离散化和图拉普拉斯问题上优于经典非Galerkin基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稀疏线性系统的可扩展求解是科学计算和图分析的瓶颈。AMG虽具有最优线性扩展性，但其性能严重受限于粗网格算子的稀疏性与收敛质量之间的权衡，经典启发式方法难以同时兼顾这两个目标。

Method: 提出RAPNet图神经网络框架，直接从稀疏代数系统学习生成稀疏且鲁棒的粗网格算子；采用层级训练策略，允许从小子图学习并泛化到百万节点域；该方法仅在求解器设置阶段执行，保持求解阶段的计算特性。

Result: 在多种PDE离散化和图拉普拉斯问题上，RAPNet优于经典非Galerkin基线方法，对多查询任务（如特征问题、时变模拟、反问题和设计问题）尤为有效。

Conclusion: RAPNet通过学习的方式有效解决了AMG中粗网格算子稀疏性与收敛质量的权衡问题，实现了从小子图到大规模问题的泛化，是一种实用且高效的神经AMG方法。

Abstract: The scalable solution of large sparse linear systems is a bottleneck in scientific computing and graph analysis. While algebraic multigrid (AMG) offers optimal linear scaling, its performance is severely constrained by the trade-off between the sparsity and convergence quality of coarse-grid operators. Classical AMG heuristics struggle to balance these objectives, often sacrificing stability or performance for sparsity. We propose RAPNet, a graph neural network (GNN) framework that resolves this trade-off by learning to generate sparse, robust coarse operators directly from the sparse algebraic system. Key to our approach is a level-wise training strategy that enables learning from small subgraphs and generalization to million-node domains, bypassing the bottlenecks of prior neural AMG attempts. RAPNet executes exclusively during the solver setup phase, ensuring that the solve phase retains its favorable computational properties. We show that our method outperforms classical non-Galerkin baselines on diverse PDE discretizations and graph Laplacians, making it particularly effective for multi-query tasks such as eigenproblems, time-dependent simulations, and inverse or design problems.

</details>


### [135] [Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection via Prototype-Based Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26857)
*Yiming Xu,Zihan Chen,Zhen Peng,Song Wang,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMoS是一个无监督的通用图异常检测框架，通过知识蒸馏从冻结的自监督GNN教师模型向混合学生模型传递正常性先验，实现零样本跨图异常检测，无需标注数据和微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用图异常检测方法依赖稀缺且昂贵的标注数据，甚至需要在推理时提供少样本支持，限制了其对多样且未见过的异常模式的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏范式，从冻结的自监督GNN教师模型向混合学生模型（共享全局分支+轻量个性化分支）蒸馏正常性先验；提出原型引导的软标签蒸馏，在共享原型空间中对齐教师与学生；推理时通过蒸馏偏差和原型几何偏差进行零样本异常检测。

Result: 大量实验表明ProMoS在跨图异常检测任务上具有有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: ProMoS为无标签、零样本的通用图异常检测提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Driven by the pressing demand for graph anomaly detection (GAD) in high-stakes domains, the generalist GAD paradigm, which trains a single detector transferable across new graphs, has recently gained growing attention. However, existing methods often rely on scarce and costly annotations for training and sometimes even require few-shot support at inference, which limits their robustness to diverse and unseen anomaly patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce ProMoS, the first unsupervised generalist GAD framework, which detects anomalies by modeling the abundant normality in unlabeled data. ProMoS adopts a knowledge-distillation paradigm to distill normality priors from a frozen self-supervised graph neural network (GNN) teacher to a mixture-of-students model with shared global and lightweight personalized branches, enabling efficient and expressive normality modeling without learning from scratch. We further propose prototype-guided soft-label distillation to align teacher and student in a shared prototype space, enhancing cross-graph generalizability. During inference, ProMoS performs zero-shot anomaly detection on unseen graphs via distillation bias and prototype geometric deviation. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness and efficiency of ProMoS, charting a practical path toward label-free, zero-shot generalist GAD.

</details>


### [136] [The Strongest Teacher Is Not Always the Best Teacher: Student-Centric Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26872)
*Zhengyu Hu,Zheyuan Xiao,Linxin Song,Fengqing Jiang,Yutai Li,Zhengyu Chen,Zhihan Xiong,Yue Liu,Junhao Lin,Yao Su,Lijie Hu,Kaize Ding,Xiao Teng,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前LLM训练中常选择最强教师模型生成学生训练数据，但本文发现教师测试能力不等同于教学能力。为此提出学生中心答案采样（SCAS）框架，通过估计学生中心学习成本从已验证的教师答案中选择最佳监督信号，实验表明该方法持续提升学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“最强教师即最佳教学者”这一隐含假设，指出即使多位教师给出相同正确答案，对特定学生而言，最强教师的答案未必是最优监督信号，旨在弥合教师能力与教学效果之间的差距。

Method: 提出Student-Centric Answer Sampling (SCAS)框架，基于token级梯度分解推导出高效的前向传播代理指标来估计学生中心学习成本，据此从多个已验证正确的教师答案中选择最适合当前学生的答案用于训练。

Result: 在30个教师模型、6个学生基础模型和8个任务上的实验表明，SCAS持续提升学生模型性能，验证了选择与学生当前状态匹配的监督信号优于单纯依赖教师能力。

Conclusion: 有效的知识蒸馏应优先考虑与学生当前学习状态匹配的监督信号，而非仅依赖教师模型的强弱，SCAS为此提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM training increasingly relies on teacher-generated supervision, from synthetic responses to reasoning traces and tool-use demonstrations. Current practice often chooses the highest-performing teacher to generate student training data, implicitly treating teacher test performance as a proxy for teaching quality. We show that this assumption can fail: even when multiple teachers provide correct answers to the same question, the answer from the strongest teacher is not necessarily the best supervision for a given student. To address this gap, we propose Student-Centric Answer Sampling (SCAS), a framework that selects from verified teacher-generated answers according to their estimated student-centric learning cost. Motivated by a token-wise gradient decomposition, we derive an efficient forward-only proxy for this cost and use it to guide answer selection during training. Experiments across 30 teacher models, 6 student base models, and 8 tasks show that SCAS consistently improves student performance, suggesting that effective distillation should prioritize supervision matched to the current student rather than teacher strength alone.

</details>


### [137] [SPHERE-JEPA: Spherical Prediction with Homogeneous Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26900)
*Léo Nicollier,Max Dunitz,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在黎曼流形上，自监督学习的最优表示几何是超球面均匀分布而非高斯分布，并据此提出SPHERE-JEPA框架，在多项基准上超越LeJEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习中表示几何的最优性刻画是根本性开放问题。LeJEPA发现欧氏空间中最优表示为各向同性高斯嵌入，但在低维流形（如超球面）上该问题尚未被探索。

Method: 通过极小极大分析证明：（1）k近邻在流形上的最优分布为均匀分布；（2）核岭回归在指数点积核与线性核下最优分布为球面均匀分布。基于此，改进LeJEPA的Cramér-Wold投影机制，强制学习超球面均匀分布而非高斯先验。

Result: SPHERE-JEPA在纹理检索mAP上提升超6%，在ImageNet-1K线性探针上ViT-B/14取得+1.8%提升，并在标准基准上一致匹配或超越LeJEPA。

Conclusion: 高斯嵌入存在非均匀密度导致的各向异性k-NN邻域偏差，超球面均匀分布是更优的表示几何先验。

Abstract: A fundamental open question in self-supervised learning (SSL) is the explicit characterization of the optimal geometry of the learned representations. Recently, LeJEPA identified isotropic Gaussian embeddings as optimal for minimizing downstream prediction risk in Euclidean spaces. However, the corresponding problem for distributions supported on lower-dimensional manifolds, such as the hypersphere, remains unexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that extending this minimax analysis to smooth distributions on Riemannian manifolds fundamentally changes the optimal solution. We show that, under a worst-case formulation, both k-nearest neighbors and kernel ridge regression induce hyperspherical uniformity. More precisely, we show that uniform distributions on manifolds are optimal for k-nearest neighbors, and that the uniform distribution on the sphere is optimal for kernel ridge regression with both the exponential dot-product kernel and the linear kernel. This theoretical insight reveals a fundamental limitation of Gaussian embeddings: their non-uniform density induces anisotropic k-NN neighborhoods, severely biasing the estimator. To correct this, we introduce SPHERE-JEPA, a theoretically grounded SSL framework. We adapt LeJEPA's Cram{é}r-Wold projection mechanism to enforce hyperspherical uniformity rather than a Gaussian prior. Empirically, SPHERE-JEPA yields significant improvements, boosting texture retrieval mAP by over 6%, while consistently matching or outperforming LeJEPA on standard benchmarks-including a +1.8% linear probing gain on ImageNet-1K (ViT-B/14).

</details>


### [138] [EEG-FM-Audit: A Systematic Evaluation and Analysis Pipeline for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26910)
*Xianheng Wang,Yige Yang,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-FM-Audit评估框架，系统分析EEG基础模型，发现经过适当调优的监督基线模型能以更少参数匹配或超越先进基础模型，而基础模型的学习范式有效性高度依赖数据集规模和架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型研究存在三个关键局限：不透明的监督基线调优、复杂学习范式的贡献未经验证、模型决策缺乏可解释性；亟需系统化的评估与分析框架。

Method: 提出EEG-FM-Audit框架，包含三个组件：(1)ASHA驱动的基准测试协议，透明优化监督基线；(2)范式级消融实验，评估不同学习范式的有效性；(3)神经生理探针(NPP)框架，检验模型是否利用了有效的EEG时空谱特性。

Result: 在四个EEG基础模型和五个监督模型、三个公开数据集上验证发现：调优后的监督基线能以更少参数匹配或超越基础模型；基础模型学习范式有效性高度依赖数据集规模和架构；NPP分析揭示了模型依赖的具体生理特征。

Conclusion: EEG-FM-Audit提供了更系统、透明的EEG基础模型评估方法，并为更具可解释性的神经解码建立了框架。

Abstract: Large EEG Foundation Models (FMs) have shown great potential for decoding EEG signals across diverse cognitive tasks. However, existing EEG-FM studies exhibit three critical limitations: opaque supervised baseline tuning, unverified contributions of complex learning paradigms, and a lack of transparency in model decision-making. To address these, we propose EEG-FM-Audit, a comprehensive evaluation and analysis pipeline designed to systematize the assessment of EEG-FMs. EEG-FM-Audit consists of three primary components: (1) an ASHA-driven benchmarking protocol that ensures fair comparisons by transparently optimizing supervised baselines; (2) paradigm-level ablation studies to evaluate the effectiveness of learning paradigms in FMs; and (3) a neurophysiological probing (NPP) framework, which explores whether FMs leverage valid temporal, spatial, and spectral EEG properties. We apply EEG-FM-Audit to four state-of-the-art EEG-FMs and five representative supervised models across three public datasets. Our results reveal that properly tuned supervised baselines can match or outperform advanced FMs, despite requiring significantly fewer parameters. Furthermore, we find that the effectiveness of learning paradigms of FMs is highly dependent on dataset scale and architecture. Finally, NPP analysis demonstrates how FMs rely on specific physiological features, establishing a framework for more interpretable neural decoding.

</details>


### [139] [When Muon Optimizer Meets Adversarial Training: A Theoretical and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26929)
*Jun Yan,Weiquan Huang,Jiankai Zuo,Yujian Mo,Xi Fang,Chengliang Wu,Zeming Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了正交化优化器Muon在对抗训练（AT）中的应用，从理论和实验两方面证明Muon通过谱范数稳定性约束抑制权重过度增长，在多种架构和威胁模型下表现优于或匹敌SGD，显著超越AdamW，揭示优化器几何结构是AT中的安全关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练默认使用SGD优化器，而自适应优化器（如AdamW）虽能提升标准训练效果却往往损害鲁棒性。近期Muon优化器在大规模训练中取得成功且内存开销与SGD相当，这引发了一个安全问题：正交化优化能否在强异质威胁模型下提升对抗训练效果？

Method: 理论分析表明Muon通过对矩阵值更新施加近似极分解（polar decomposition），对更新施加谱范数稳定性上界，在不显式收缩学习权重的前提下限制训练动态中谱的失控增长。实验方面，在五种架构、三种ℓ_p威胁模型（ℓ_∞、ℓ_1、ℓ_2）及其联合威胁下进行系统对比。

Result: Muon在CNN架构上与SGD竞争力相当，在CNN和ViT上均显著优于AdamW。正交化更新在对抗训练中具有明确的适用场景优势。

Conclusion: 优化器几何结构是对抗训练中一个被忽视但至关重要的安全因素，正交化优化器设计应被视为对抗训练的安全关键组件。

Abstract: Adversarial training (AT) remains one of the most reliable empirical defenses against adversarial attacks. Its robustness critically depends on how the underlying min-max objective is optimized. In practice, Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) optimizer remains the default optimization choice for AT, whereas adaptive optimizers often improve standard training but may yield inferior robustness. Recently, the Muon optimizer, which orthogonalizes matrix-valued updates via an approximate polar decomposition, has achieved notable success in large-scale training at a memory cost comparable to SGD. This raises a security-relevant question: \textit{can orthogonalized optimization improve AT under strong and heterogeneous threat models?} Focusing on this problem, we conduct a comprehensive theoretical and empirical study. Theoretically, we show that Muon imposes a spectral-norm stability ceiling on matrix updates, limiting uncontrolled spectral growth in the training dynamics without explicitly shrinking the learned weights. Empirically, across five architectures and three $\ell_p$ threat models ($\ell_\infty$, $\ell_1$, $\ell_2$) and their union, Muon is competitive with SGD on CNNs and substantially outperforms AdamW on both CNNs and ViTs. These results identify optimizer geometry as a security-relevant factor in adversarial training, while clarifying the empirical regimes in which orthogonalized updates are beneficial. Overall, our findings highlight optimizer design as a security-critical component of AT.

</details>


### [140] [RLVR Datasets and Where to Find Them: Tracing Data Lineage for Better Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26971)
*Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Weijie Liu,Chenming Tang,Sanwoo Lee,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS框架，系统性追溯RLVR数据集至20个原子源（覆盖99.7%的145万实例），揭示数据碎片化与污染问题；基于此提出DAPO++数据集与SCA归因原则，通过比较每原子源的RL检查点与基模型计算样本边际效用，设计复合质量分数Q，在Qwen3系列模型上验证其与下游RLVR性能的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR数据集的激增导致谱系崩溃（provenance collapse），现有数据集来源不清、碎片化严重，亟需系统性溯源方法以理解数据生态并解决数据污染问题。

Method: 1) ATLAS：通过谱系感知搜索将1.45M实例回溯至20个原子源；2) SCA：源级反事实归因，通过对比每个原子源训练的RL检查点与共享基模型的性能差异，衡量样本边际效用；3) 基于SCA信号设计复合质量分数Q。

Result: ATLAS成功归因99.7%实例至20个原子源；揭示大多数RLVR数据集共享少数上游源，真实新数据极少且存在污染风险；DAPO++在Qwen3系列模型上持续提升留出基准性能；Q分数与下游RLVR训练效果强相关。

Conclusion: 系统性溯源与反事实归因能有效应对RLVR数据谱系崩溃问题，SCA引导的去污染数据集构建和Q分数可作为RLVR数据筛选的有效工具。

Abstract: The proliferation of Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) datasets has exacerbated provenance collapse due to unclear lineage among existing datasets. To bridge this fragmented RLVR data landscape, we propose Atomic-source Tracing via Lineage-Aware Search (ATLAS), a systematic framework for tracing RLVR datasets back to their atomic sources, attributing over 99.7% of 1.45M instances to 20 atomic sources. Our analysis reveals that most RLVR datasets are variants of a small set of shared upstream sources, with few introducing genuinely new data, and many facing data contamination risks. These findings naturally motivate us to curate a new RLVR dataset, DAPO++, and to benchmark existing datasets from a lineage-aware perspective. To this end, we propose Source-level Counterfactual Attribution (SCA) as a guiding principle to curate a decontaminated training dataset with concentrated learning signals. Essentially, SCA measures a sample's marginal utility by comparing per-atomic-source RL checkpoints against a shared base model. Building upon these attribution signals, we further design a composite dataset quality score Q that strongly correlates with downstream RLVR performance. Experiments on Qwen3 series models verify that DAPO++ consistently improves performance on held-out benchmarks, while Q reliably predicts downstream RLVR training effectiveness. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/Celine-hxy/ATLAS.

</details>


### [141] [TED: Related Party Transaction guided Tax Evasion Detection on Heterogeneous Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26984)
*Yiming Xu,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Jiaxiang Wang,Hua Wei,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于异构图神经网络的新型逃税检测方法，利用层次注意力机制捕获关联方交易组中的深层结构语义信息，在真实税务数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逃税检测方法主要依赖公司统计特征，未能充分利用税务场景中丰富的交互信息，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 将税务场景建模为异构图，利用异构且复杂的关联方交易组过滤低层噪声信息，并设计层次注意力机制捕获深层结构和语义信息。

Result: 在真实税务系统的两个人工标注数据集上，该方法显著优于当前最先进的逃税检测方法。

Conclusion: 异构图神经网络结合层次注意力机制能够有效挖掘税务场景中的复杂交互信息，提升逃税检测性能。

Abstract: Tax evasion causes severe losses of government revenues and disturbs the economic order of fair competition. To help alleviate this problem, the latest tax evasion detection solutions utilize expert knowledge to extract features and then train classifiers to determine whether a company is suspected of tax evasion. However, existing solutions mainly focus on the statistical features of the company, but fail to exploit the rich interactive information in tax scenarios, which affect the detection performance. In this paper, we first model the tax scenario as a heterogeneous graph and study the tax evasion detection problem under the heterogeneous graph model. To improve the performance of tax evasion detection, a novel graph neural network model is proposed to extract the comprehensive information of heterogeneous graphs. Specifically, we use heterogeneous and complex related party transaction groups to filter low-level noise information. Moreover, a hierarchical attention mechanism is designed to capture the deeper structure and semantic information hidden in the related party transaction group. We apply our method to the real risk management system of the tax bureau, and evaluate it on two human-labeled real-world tax datasets. The results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art in the tax evasion detection task.

</details>


### [142] [Probabilistic Recurrent Intention Switching Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26998)
*Wenyuan Sheng,Hao Zhu,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM模型，用轻量级循环网络替代马尔可夫链或固定历史窗口，实现多意图逆强化学习中的意图切换建模，在非马尔可夫网格世界、小鼠迷宫和BridgeData V2机器人操作任务上取得最优似然，并恢复出可命名的、时间连贯的意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IRL假设单一平稳奖励函数，无法捕获序列内的目标切换；现有多意图IRL方法要么假设意图转移为无记忆马尔可夫链，要么通过手动状态增强和固定历史窗口建模，灵活性不足。

Method: 提出概率循环意图切换模型（PRISM），用轻量级循环神经网络将观测历史映射到每一步的意图分布；证明EM目标可精确分解为独立的每意图奖励子问题，每个子问题有闭式解，E-step复杂度为O(nK)，无需变分近似。

Result: 在非马尔可夫网格世界、小鼠迷宫和BridgeData V2机器人操作三个任务上，PRISM均取得最高的保留对数似然，并能从无标签演示中恢复出可命名、时间连贯的意图。

Conclusion: 离散目标切换在生物和人工智能体中普遍存在；PRISM为大规模机器人操作中的多意图IRL提供了首个成功应用。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) recovers reward functions from observed behavior, yet traditional methods assume a single stationary reward that cannot capture goal switching within an episode. Recent multi-intention IRL methods address this by segmenting trajectories, but model intention transitions as either a memoryless Markov chain or via manual state augmentation with a fixed history window. We propose the Probabilistic Recurrent Intention Switching Model (PRISM), which replaces both mechanisms with a lightweight recurrent network that maps observation history to a per-step intention distribution. We prove that the resulting EM objective decomposes exactly into independent per-intention reward subproblems, each solvable in closed form, yielding an $\mathcal{O}(nK)$ E-step with no variational approximation. We evaluate PRISM on a non-Markovian gridworld, a mouse labyrinth, and BridgeData~V2 robotic manipulation, the first large-scale robotic application of multi-intention IRL. Across all settings PRISM achieves the highest held-out log-likelihood while recovering nameable, temporally coherent intentions from unlabeled demonstrations, suggesting that discrete goal switching is present in both biological and artificial agents.

</details>


### [143] [SCENT: Aligning Mass Spectra with Molecular Structure for Olfactory Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27009)
*Ziqi Zhang,Eunyeong Jin,Miguel Vasco,Farzaneh Taleb,Nona Rajabi,Alexandra Gutmann,Jonathan Williams,Antônio H. Ribeiro,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCENT框架，通过跨模态对比学习将电子电离质谱（EI-MS）表征与化学结构嵌入对齐，在无需显式化学结构的情况下实现与结构模型相当的嗅觉预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嗅觉预测方法依赖推理时所需的显式化学结构，而在实际传感场景中无法获取这种结构信息，因此需要探索替代输入模态。

Method: 提出SCENT（Spectrum-to-Chemical Embedding alignmeNT），一个多模态对比学习框架，将EI-MS表征与预训练的化学结构嵌入进行对齐，推理时仅需质谱数据。

Result: 在多标签气味描述词预测任务中，SCENT显著优于仅使用质谱的基线方法，性能与基于结构的模型相当；学习到的表征能更好地逼近连续的人类感知评分，并泛化到真实实验室测量的质谱数据。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐是将分析质谱数据锚定到化学语义中的有效策略，为实际传感场景中的嗅觉预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Predicting human olfactory perception from molecular structure has seen remarkable progress, yet these approaches require explicit chemical structure at inference, which is not available in practical sensing settings. We address this gap by exploring direct electron ionization mass spectrometry (EI-MS), a sensing technique that acquires chemically informative fragmentation fingerprints in seconds, as an alternative input modality for olfactory prediction. We contribute Spectrum-to-Chemical Embedding alignmeNT (SCENT), a multi-modal contrastive learning framework that aligns EI-MS representations with pretrained chemical structure embeddings, while requiring only mass spectra at inference. On the multi-label odor descriptor prediction task, SCENT significantly outperforms MS-only baselines and achieves performance comparable to structure-based models, despite requiring no explicit molecular structure at test time. The learned representations also better approximate continuous human perceptual ratings and generalize to real-world lab-measured spectra, suggesting that cross-modal alignment is an effective strategy for grounding analytical spectra in chemical semantics.

</details>


### [144] [SQARL: A Size-Agnostic Reinforcement Learning approach for Circuit Allocation in Distributed Quantum Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27027)
*Víctor Carballo,Júlia López-Closa,Mario Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Transformer的灵活分布式量子计算架构，无需重新训练即可处理任意数量的量子比特和核心，在分配成本上比先前强化学习方法降低33%，并缩小了与最先进启发式算法（HQA）的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算中，最小化核心间通信成本的量子比特分配问题至关重要。现有强化学习方法缺乏灵活性，需随硬件配置变化重新训练，且解质量不及非学习方法。因此需要一种兼具灵活性和高性能的学习方法。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的灵活架构，可处理任意数量的量子比特和核心而无需重新训练。通过深度强化学习训练分配策略。

Result: 该策略在Cuccaro Adder上相比HQA实现33%的分配成本降低，随机电路上平均降低25%，并持续优于先前强化学习方法，缩小了与HQA的差距。

Conclusion: 基于学习的方法能够有效匹配手工启发式算法的性能，这是向实际场景应用迈出的关键一步。

Abstract: The scaling of quantum processors is currently limited by technical challenges such as decoherence and cross-talk. As the number of qubits grows, interference increases the computational noise. Distributed quantum computing addresses these limitations by interconnecting smaller, easier-to-handle quantum processors (cores), but it introduces the challenge of minimizing slow, error-prone inter-core communication. The task of distributing quantum circuits across cores while minimizing communication costs is known as the Qubit Allocation problem. This work focuses on developing a deep learning approach to this problem, emphasizing flexibility to quantum hardware topology and improving state-of-the-art performance.
  Heuristic and non-learning algorithms, such as the Hungarian Qubit Allocation (HQA), currently represent the state of the art. Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches leverage learned allocation policies but often lack flexibility, requiring retraining when hardware configurations change, and they fall short of the solution quality achieved by non-learning methods. However, learning mechanisms could outperform human-crafted heuristics.
  To overcome these limitations, this work proposes a flexible, transformer-based architecture that can handle arbitrary numbers of qubits and cores without retraining. Results show that the trained policy consistently outperforms the previous RL state of the art and narrows the gap between RL and HQA for the most common circuits. It achieves a 33% reduction in allocation cost relative to the HQA for the Cuccaro Adder and 25% on average for random circuits. These findings show that learning-based approaches can effectively match the performance of hand-crafted heuristics, a crucial step towards their application in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [Less is More: Early Stopping Rollout for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27028)
*Zhou Ziheng,Jiaqi Li,Huacong Tang,Ying Nian Wu,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现"离策略教师衰退"问题：在策略蒸馏中，教师对基于学生早期轨迹（离策略）生成的后续token评分能力下降。提出**早停 rollout（ESR）**策略，仅限制rollout生成前K个响应token，在多个维度超越完整rollout OPD性能，效率更高、训练更稳定，甚至有时超越教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到策略蒸馏中存在"Off-policy Teacher Decay"问题：学生早期轨迹作为上下文对教师是离策略的，导致教师对后续token产生纠正性评分的能力衰减，退化到预训练阶段的token补全行为。

Method: 提出Early Stopping Rollout (ESR)，一种简单有效的蒸馏策略，限制rollout生成仅在前K个响应token，避免离策略上下文导致的教师评分能力衰退。

Result: ESR在模型规模、家族、任务和训练范式上均超越完整rollout OPD性能；GPU效率和训练稳定性更高，尤其在跨模型族场景下；甚至有时超越教师模型性能。

Conclusion: 发现"Cascading Alignment"和"Sub-mode Commitment"效应解释ESR的有效性；ESR的位置token选择策略不能完全用KL散度和熵信号解释。

Abstract: On-policy distillation has recently emerged as a promising alternative to standard sequence-level imitation, training a student by scoring its own rollouts with a teacher model. However, we observe ``Off-policy Teacher Decay'' problem in this paradigm: for the later tokens, with student's earlier trajectory as context that is off-policy to the teacher, the teacher's ability to produce a corrective score would decay, and may fall back to token-completion behavior learned in the pre-training stage. We empirically verify this problem, and we propose Early Stopping Rollout (ESR) to fix it: a simple yet effective distillation strategy that simply restricts the rollout generation to the first
  response tokens. We show that ESR both surpasses the full rollout OPD performance across model size, family, tasks and training regime, and exhibit much higher GPU efficiency and training stability, especially under cross model family scenarios. We further investigate the mechanism behind this surprising performance and discovered "Cascading Alignment" and "Sub-mode Commitment" effect of ESR that may explain why it works effectively and even sometimes exceeding the teacher model performance. Besides, we show that this position-based token selection strategy cannot be fully explainable by KL divergence and entropy signals.

</details>


### [146] [Learning Dynamic Graph Representations through Timespan View Contrasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27063)
*Yiming Xu,Zhen Peng,Bin Shi,Xu Hua,Bo Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLDG和CLDG++框架，通过引入时间平移不变性这一归纳偏置，利用对比学习在动态图上建模时间演化，同时支持节点分类和动态图异常检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督图表示学习主要依赖静态图中的节点特征和拓扑性质，忽略了真实图数据中的时间成分（如边的时间戳），缺乏对动态图时间演化的有效建模。

Method: 基于时间平移不变性归纳偏置，通过对比学习鼓励节点在不同时间跨度下保持局部一致的时间平移不变性。CLDG利用显式拓扑链接，CLDG++额外引入图扩散捕捉全局上下文关联，并设计多尺度对比学习目标（局部-局部、局部-全局、全局-全局）。

Result: CLDG和CLDG++在节点分类和动态图异常检测任务上均表现出色；CLDG通过隐式利用时间线索显著降低时间和空间复杂度。

Conclusion: 时间平移不变性是一种有效的动态图表示学习归纳偏置；对比学习框架可无缝集成动态图异常检测任务，在金融、网络安全和医疗等高风险领域具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: The rich information underlying graphs has inspired further investigation of unsupervised graph representation. Existing studies mainly depend on node features and topological properties within static graphs to create self-supervised signals, neglecting the temporal components carried by real-world graph data, such as timestamps of edges. To overcome this limitation, this paper explores how to model temporal evolution on dynamic graphs elegantly. Specifically, we introduce a new inductive bias, namely temporal translation invariance, which illustrates the tendency of the identical node to keep similar labels across different timespans. Based on this assumption, we develop a dynamic graph representation framework CLDG that encourages the node to maintain locally consistent temporal translation invariance through contrastive learning on different timespans. Except for standard CLDG which only considers explicit topological links, our further proposed CLDG++ additionally employs graph diffusion to uncover global contextual correlations between nodes, and designs a multi-scale contrastive learning objective composed of local-local, local-global, and global-global contrasts to enhance representation capabilities. Interestingly, by measuring the consistency between different timespans to shape anomaly indicators, CLDG and CLDG++ are seamlessly integrated with the task of spotting anomalies on dynamic graphs, which has broad applications in many high-impact domains, such as finance, cybersecurity, and healthcare. Experiments demonstrate that CLDG and CLDG++ both exhibit desirable performance in downstream tasks including node classification and dynamic graph anomaly detection. Moreover, CLDG significantly reduces time and space complexity by implicitly exploiting temporal cues instead of complicated sequence models.

</details>


### [147] [Learning to Orchestrate Agents under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27073)
*Mary Chriselda Antony Oliver,Lan Jiang,Aaron Bundi Anampiu,Elaf Almahmoud,Francesco Quinzan,Umang Bhatt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BOT-Orch框架，将异构Agent自适应编排建模为带正则化的赌博机问题，利用最优传输距离正则化实现不确定下的最优委托决策，理论保证O(√T)遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent编排方法主要关注性能或成本，未在编排层面显式建模Agent可靠性和输出分布的不确定性，导致在异构、非平稳环境下难以做出鲁棒的委托决策。

Method: 将编排问题转化为赌博机问题，用最优传输(OT)距离衡量Agent输出分布与任务参考分布之间的对齐程度，以此作为正则化项指导委托决策。

Result: 理论证明算法享有O(√T)遗憾界，且能对均值相同但分布对齐不同的Agent诱导出偏好排序；实验在合成对抗性任务分配场景中优于标准赌博机基线和启发式方法。

Conclusion: BOT-Orch通过分布对齐正则化有效处理了异构Agent编排中的不确定性问题，为自适应编排提供了轻量级且具有理论保障的解决方案。

Abstract: Adaptive orchestration of heterogeneous agents requires making sequential delegation decisions under uncertain and evolving agent behaviour, e.g., coordinating specialised AI models with varying reliability, cost, and response quality. While prior work on agent orchestration focuses on performance or cost, uncertainty in agent reliability and output distributions is typically not modelled explicitly at the orchestration level. In this work, we study the problem of adaptive orchestration of heterogeneous agents under uncertainty, where a meta-controller must decide when to delegate to an agent, accounting for reliability, cost, and uncertainty. We propose BOT-Orch, a lightweight framework that recasts orchestration as a bandit problem over agents, regularized by OT distances between agent output distributions and task-specific reference distributions. We show that the regularised orchestration enjoys $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under standard assumptions, and provably induces preference ordering among agents with identical mean rewards but differing distributional alignment. Empirically, we demonstrate that BOT-Orch outperforms standard bandit and heuristic baselines in synthetic but adversarial task allocation settings with heterogeneous, non-i.i.d. agent behaviour.

</details>


### [148] [Two Speeds of Learning: A Representation-Readout Decomposition of Grokking and Double Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27078)
*Chi-Ning Chou,Oscar Uzdelewicz,Neng-Chun Chiu,Yao-Yuan Yang,SueYeon Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种表征-读出分解框架，用于解释深度学习训练中出现的异常泛化现象（如grokking和epoch-wise double descent），通过分析编码器中的表征学习与最终分类器的读出校准这两个竞争过程，揭示了其背后的动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监控深度学习泛化行为主要依赖训练损失和准确率，但grokking（训练损失下降后测试性能延迟提升）和epoch-wise double descent（测试错误率先升后降）等现象无法用这些标准信号解释。已有解释往往任务特定，缺乏跨任务和架构的通用分析框架。

Method: 利用表征几何、神经切线核（NTK）和线性探测等工具，将学习动力学分解为编码器的表征学习过程和最终分类器的读出校准过程，分析这两个竞争过程的相对速度波动。

Result: 1) 两个过程在整个训练过程中同时活跃，其相对速度的波动导致看似异常的泛化动态；2) 在grokking中，读出在grokking发生前偏向训练集，表征学习是渐进而非缺失的，这与lazy-to-rich解释相矛盾；3) 框架能诊断虚假泛化与真实泛化的区分标志，如MNIST grokking和epoch-wise double descent中的异常源于表征退化和读出错位。

Conclusion: 表征-读出分解框架为理解学习动力学和揭示可解释性研究中的底层算法提供了一个自上而下的通用分析框架。

Abstract: Training loss and accuracy are the standard signals used to monitor generalization during deep neural network training. Two well-documented phenomena complicate this picture: in grokking, train loss falls rapidly while test performance improves abruptly only after a long delay; in epoch-wise double descent, train loss decreases monotonically while test loss or error rises and falls. Existing accounts are often task-specific, and a task-agnostic analysis framework for diagnosing and explaining these phenomena across realistic tasks and architectures is missing. We address this challenge by analyzing two competing processes that underlie learning dynamics: representation learning in the encoder and readout calibration in the final classifier. Using tools from representational geometry, neural tangent kernels, and linear probing, we show that both processes are active throughout training, with the fluctuations of their relative speed giving rise to seemingly anomalous generalization dynamics. Applying the representation-readout decomposition to grokking across a wide range of tasks and architectures, we find that the readout is train-biased before grokking onset, and representation learning is gradual but not absent, contrary to the lazy-to-rich account. The framework further provides diagnostic signatures distinguishing spurious from genuine generalization: in a previously reported MNIST grokking example and an epoch-wise double descent example, apparent delayed or non-monotone generalization is shown to arise from representation degradation and readout misalignment induced by non-standard training recipes. Together, these results establish the representation-readout decomposition as a top-down framework for understanding learning dynamics and revealing underlying algorithms for interpretability research.

</details>


### [149] [Trust Region Q Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27079)
*Yonghoon Dong,Kyungmin Lee,Changyeon Kim,Jaehyuk Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRQAM通过投影对偶下降自适应控制预训练流策略的路径空间KL散度，解决了基于critic的离线策略强化学习中的优化不稳定和模型崩溃问题，在50个OGBench任务中离线RL成功率达68%，远超最强基线46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略强化学习方法（如QAM）在微调预训练流策略时，因critic误差在病态条件下被放大，导致优化不稳定和模型崩溃。

Method: 提出Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM)，通过投影对偶下降自适应控制路径空间KL散度，优化SOC动力学中的信任区域参数λ，并理论证明路径空间KL可表示为λ的闭式函数。

Result: 在50个OGBench任务中，TRQAM在离线RL和离线到在线RL中均优于现有方法，离线RL成功率达68%，显著超过最强基线（46%）。

Conclusion: TRQAM通过精确控制与预训练流策略的偏差，实现了稳定的离线策略RL，有效缓解了critic引导改进的脆弱性。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning of pretrained flow policies remains challenging due to the instability of optimization arising from the multi-step sampling process. Recently, Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) addressed this issue by reformulating into a memoryless stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem with a learned critic. However, QAM inherits a fundamental fragility of critic-guided improvement: small critic errors are amplified when critics are ill-conditioned, often leading to model collapse. This paper introduces Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM), a stable off-policy fine-tuning algorithm that adaptively controls the path-space KL with pretrained flow policies through projected dual descent. Specifically, we optimize the trust-region parameter $λ$ in SOC dynamics, and theoretically show that the path-space KL can be represented by a closed-form function of $λ$. As a result, our method can precisely control the exact deviation from pretrained flow policies, achieving stable off-policy RL. Through experiments on 50 OGBench tasks, TRQAM consistently outperforms prior arts in both offline RL and offline-to-online RL. In particular, TRQAM achieves an overall success rate of 68% in offline RL, substantially improves the strongest baseline at 46%.

</details>


### [150] [ReMoE: Boosting Expert Reuse through Router Fine-Tuning in Memory-Constrained MoE LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27081)
*Xiongwei Zhu,Xiaojian Liao,Tianyang Jiang,Yusen Zhang,Liang Wang,Limin Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReMoE通过微调路由器使MoE模型的路由决策偏向近期选中的专家，提升专家的时间局部性，从而减少缓存未命中时的外部存储访问开销，在DeepSeek和Qwen模型上实现26%的专家重用提升，并在实际系统中带来8.4%的吞吐量提升和1.77-1.99倍的解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的推理场景中，MoE模型只能缓存少量专家，未命中缓存的专家需从慢速外部存储获取，导致频繁的缓存驱逐和大量I/O开销。

Method: 提出ReMoE，一种路由器微调框架，通过偏向近期选中的专家来产生时间上稳定的路由决策，使路由更好地匹配缓存局部性约束，增加短时间范围内的专家重用。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen模型上，专家重用提升26%且下游任务性能保持不变；在vLLM GPU-CPU专家卸载场景下输出吞吐量提升8.4%；在llama.cpp于Jetson Orin NX上TPOT降低43.6-49.8%，对应1.77-1.99倍解码加速。

Conclusion: ReMoE在不增加推理计算量的前提下，通过提升专家时间局部性有效减少存储访问开销，显著提升MoE模型在内存受限场景下的推理效率。

Abstract: Fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models sparsely activate only a subset of experts per token, reducing activated computation while maintaining high model capacity. However, in memory-constrained inference scenarios, only a small set of experts can be cached. Experts not in the cache must be fetched from slow external storage (e.g., UFS), leading to frequent evictions and substantial I/O overhead.
  We propose ReMoE, a router fine-tuning framework designed to boost token-wise expert reuse. ReMoE biases the router toward recently selected experts, producing temporally stable routing that better matches cache locality constraints. By increasing short-horizon expert reuse, ReMoE reduces expert fetches from storage without adding inference-time computation.
  Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen models show that ReMoE improves expert reuse by 26% while maintaining downstream task performance. Real-system evaluations further confirm these benefits, improving output throughput by 8.4% under vLLM GPU-CPU expert offloading and reducing TPOT by 43.6-49.8% under llama.cpp on Jetson Orin NX, corresponding to a 1.77-1.99$\times$ decode speedup across diverse workloads. Checkpoints and usage instructions are available at https://github.com/BUAA-OSCAR/ReMoE.

</details>


### [151] [Adversarial Dual On-Policy Distillation from Expressive Flow-based Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27095)
*Zhenglin Wan,Jingxuan Wu,Xingrui Yu,Chubin Zhang,Mingcong Lei,Bo An,Ivor W. Tsang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FA-OPD是一种对抗性双在线策略蒸馏方法，通过从演示中学习流匹配教师，并结合奖励蒸馏和动作蒸馏两种互补信号，在机器人导航、操作和 locomotion 任务上显著优于现有方法，尤其在噪声或有限演示下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于演示学习的行为克隆方法（如扩散/流匹配策略）仅限于离线监督学习，策略仅在专家状态上训练，无法在自身访问的状态上获得纠正信号。在线策略蒸馏虽能缓解该问题，但通常假设存在一个固定的强教师，这在仅含演示的控制场景中不可用。

Method: 提出FA-OPD方法，包含：1）从演示中学习流匹配教师，并与轻量级MLP学生协同训练；2）教师提供两种互补信号——奖励通道通过状态-动作对的专家相似度目标驱动在线探索和长程策略优化，动作通道在学生访问的状态上提供稠密局部目标以稳定利用；3）两种蒸馏机制耦合，使奖励蒸馏实现超越逐点演示的泛化，动作蒸馏将探索锚定在专家行为附近。

Result: 在六个机器人导航、操作和 locomotion 基准任务上，FA-OPD击败了强基线方法，并在噪声或有限演示条件下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过对抗性双在线策略蒸馏，将流匹配教师与轻量级学生结合，利用奖励和动作两种蒸馏信号的互补优势，有效解决了演示控制中缺乏在线纠正信号的问题，显著提升了策略性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations in embodied control is often cast as behavioral cloning, and recent diffusion or flow-matching policies improve this paradigm by modeling multi-modal expert actions. Yet these methods remain offline supervised learners: the policy is trained only on expert states and receives no corrective signal on the states it actually visits. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a natural remedy, but standard OPD assumes a strong fixed teacher, which is unavailable in demonstration-only control. We propose \textbf{FA-OPD}, an \emph{adversarial dual on-policy distillation} method in which a Flow Matching (FM) teacher is learned from demonstrations and co-trained with a lightweight MLP student. The teacher provides two complementary signals on student rollouts. The reward channel learns an expert-likeness objective over state-action pairs and drives online exploration through long-horizon policy optimization. The action channel supplies dense local targets at student-visited states, stabilizing exploitation. FA-OPD couples them so that reward distillation enables generalization beyond point-wise demonstrations, while action distillation keeps exploration anchored near expert-like behavior. Across six robot navigation, manipulation, and locomotion benchmarks, FA-OPD beats strong baselines and shows much stronger robustness under noisy or limited demonstrations.

</details>


### [152] [High-Quality Synthetic Financial Time-Series using a GAN-Diffusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27113)
*Giuseppe Masi,Andrea Coletta,Novella Bartolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种质量感知的生成框架，结合条件生成对抗网络（CoMeTS-GAN）与扩散模型，利用GAN的Critic作为质量评估模块引导扩散过程，以生成更真实、符合金融时间序列典型事实和资产间相关结构的多变量股票市场数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构越来越多地采用合成数据来应对数据稀缺和生成反事实市场场景，但现有通用架构难以复现金融时间序列的全部统计属性（即典型事实），尤其是跨资产相关结构。

Method: 提出CoMeTS-GAN（相关多变量时间序列GAN），一种条件生成对抗网络，用于联合生成相关股票的中价和成交量时间序列；然后将该GAN架构融入扩散模型，利用GAN的Critic作为质量评估模块，通过强制学习到的相关结构来引导扩散过程。

Result: 实验验证表明，该框架相比领先的生成架构，更有效地捕捉了股票市场的典型事实，并能更好地建模资产间的相关结构。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了轻量级且响应迅速的股票市场模拟方案，通过显式建模跨资产相关结构，实现了更真实的合成金融时间序列生成。

Abstract: In recent years, financial institutions and firms have increasingly adopted synthetic data to address data scarcity and to generate counterfactual market scenarios. However, reproducing all the statistical properties of financial time series, commonly known as stylized facts, remains an open challenge for many existing general-purpose architectures. In this paper, we present a quality-aware generative framework that combines two classes of generative methods, demonstrating how their integration addresses existing limitations while enhancing the realism of synthetic data. Specifically, we first introduce CoMeTS-GAN (Correlated Multivariate Time Series GAN), a Conditional Generative Adversarial Network (C-GAN) designed to jointly generate mid-price and volume time-series for correlated stocks. We then show how our GAN architecture can be incorporated into state-of-the-art diffusion models to enhance the quality of generated correlation structures. Specifically, the GAN's Critic serves as a quality evaluation module that guides the diffusion process, enforcing learned correlation structures in the generated time-series. Our framework offers a lightweight and responsive solution for realistic stock market simulation, explicitly modeling inter-asset correlation structures. We experimentally validate our framework against leading generative architectures, showing that it more effectively captures the stylized facts of stock markets and models inter-asset correlations.

</details>


### [153] [DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference for Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27130)
*John Donaghy,Shikhar Rastogi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEI是一个分布式质量-多样性（QD）搜索框架，利用异构大语言模型（LLMs）作为变异算子，通过非阻塞集体通信在集群节点间协作。在Core War基准测试中，四节点异构集成相比单节点基线在QD-Score上提升124%，覆盖率提升28%，证明了模型多样性而非单纯并行化是分布式LLM-QD搜索性能提升的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索模型多样性（而非同质并行化）在分布式LLM驱动的质量-多样性搜索中是否以及如何带来性能增益。

Method: 提出DEI框架，将异构LLM作为变异算子部署在分布式节点上，节点间通过非阻塞集体通信共享局部最优解，形成跨模型的对抗压力。在Core War领域以Redcode程序对战为评估场景，比较异构集成、同质集成与单节点基线。

Result: 四节点异构集成（GPT-5.4-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.2, Claude Haiku 4.5）在等LLM调用预算下，QD-Score达45.90（单节点基线20.46，提升124%），覆盖率达80.6%（单节点基线63.0%，提升28%），且在所有模型族上均优于同质集成。

Conclusion: 首次提供经验证据表明，模型多样性而非单纯并行化是分布式LLM-QD搜索性能增益的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: We present DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference, a distributed Quality-Diversity (QD) search framework that assigns heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) as mutation operators across peer nodes communicating with non-blocking collective operations. Unlike homogeneous parallel search, which replicates a single model's inductive biases across all workers, DEI treats each LLM's distinct creative prior as a complementary source of behavioral novelty. Extending the Digital Red Queen framework with DEI, nodes share local optimal solutions at the end of each round to seed the next round's population. This creates cross-model adversarial pressure that drives robustness beyond intra-model self-play. Evaluated on the Core War domain, a competitive programming benchmark in which Redcode warrior programs battle inside a simulated machine, a four-node heterogeneous ensemble (GPT-5.4-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.2, and Claude Haiku 4.5) achieves 124 percent higher merged-archive QD-Score (45.90 vs. 20.46) and 28 percent higher coverage (80.6 percent vs. 63.0 percent of cells) than a single-node baseline at equal total LLM-call budget. The heterogeneous ensemble also outperforms an equally-budgeted homogeneous ensemble on QD-Score, coverage, and held-out solution generality across all four model families. These results provide the first empirical evidence that model diversity, not merely parallelism, is the key driver of gain in distributed LLM-based QD search.

</details>


### [154] [Deep-layer limit and stability analysis of the basic forward-backward-splitting induced network (II): learning problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27133)
*Xuan Lin,Chunlin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由基本前向-后向分裂（FBS）算法展开得到的深度神经网络，建立了该网络训练问题在深层极限系统下的Γ-收敛性，证明网络最优参数的任意聚点都是极限系统学习问题的解，并给出了学习问题的扰动稳定性定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于迭代优化方案和数值微分方程导出的深度展开神经网络在数据科学中受到广泛关注，其中许多重要网络结构源自基本的前向-后向分裂（FBS）算法。本文旨在从理论上分析FBS诱导网络的学习问题，特别是其收敛性和稳定性。

Method: 采用差异/微分包含公式化方法，在温和假设下建立FBS诱导网络训练问题到深层极限系统学习问题的Γ-收敛性论证，证明网络最优学习参数的任意聚点都是极限系统学习问题的解；同时进行了学习问题的扰动稳定性定性分析，并通过简单数值实验验证主要收敛结果。

Result: 建立了基本FBS诱导网络训练问题到深层极限系统学习问题的Γ-收敛性，表明网络最优参数的任意聚点均为极限系统学习问题的解；给出了学习问题扰动稳定性的定性分析结果；数值实验验证了主要收敛结果。

Conclusion: 本文从理论层面深化了对FBS诱导展开网络的理解，通过Γ-收敛性建立了有限层网络与深层极限系统学习问题之间的联系，为深度展开网络的设计与分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Deep unfolding neural networks derived from iterative optimization schemes and numerical ordinary/partial differential equations (ODEs/PDEs) have attracted much attention in data science over the last decade. Therein, numerous important network architectures were constructed from the basic forward-backward-splitting (FBS) algorithm. In this paper, we continue our research on the most basic FBS-induced network, an architecture unrolled from the original FBS algorithm by incorporating direct parameter relaxations. Following the difference/differential inclusion formulations in our previous forward system analyses, we here consider some theoretical aspects of corresponding learning problems. Under some mild assumptions, we establish a general convergence property of the training problem of the basic FBS-induced network to the learning problem of the deep-layer limit system, implying a $Γ$-convergence argument showing that any cluster point of the optimal learning parameters for the network is a solution to the learning problem of the deep-layer limit system. A qualitative analysis of perturbation stabilities of these learning problems is also presented. A simple numerical experiment is conducted to validate our main general convergence result.

</details>


### [155] [LUCoS: Latent Unsupervised Context Selection for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27254)
*Oroel Ipas,Guillermo Gomez-Trenado,Rocío Romero-Zaliz,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对表格基础模型（如TabPFN）在低标签场景下的冷启动问题，提出LUCoS方法：利用无监督预训练PFN的潜在嵌入空间替代原始特征空间进行上下文选择，通过选择代表性medoids构建标注集。在67个数据集上，LUCoS在AUC、ACC、F1等指标上排名第一，且在小预算时覆盖度起主要作用，预算增大时表示空间质量成为决定性因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低标签表格学习中，上下文选择直接决定TabPFN等表格基础模型的预测性能。有监督实验表明精心选择的标注集远优于随机选择，但冷启动（无标签可用时）场景下的实例选择问题尚未被充分研究。原始表格空间缺乏自然度量，异构类型、混合尺度和非线性交互使得原始空间距离不可靠，多数数据集上原始空间选择效果甚至低于随机。

Method: 提出LUCoS（Latent Unsupervised Context Selection），核心思想是用无监督Prior-Fitted Network（PFN）生成的嵌入向量替代原始特征，在潜在空间中通过选择代表性medoids构建上下文集。该方法分为两步：1）用无监督PFN将原始表格数据映射到潜在嵌入空间；2）在该空间中选取medoids作为待标注实例。

Result: 在67个OpenML-CC18数据集上，跨越6个低标签预算设置，LUCoS在平均AUC、ACC和F1指标上均排名第一，结论在各指标和数据集层面稳健性检验中保持一致。增益分解揭示：小预算时主要收益来自确保覆盖度；预算增大时，决定性因素是衡量覆盖度的表示空间质量。
